# vision-web

Angular 21 SPA (driving adapter): the whole product UI — operator cockpit, manager dashboard, tactical map, asset/device management, replay, CV training — talking only to vision-api's REST/SSE surface, built into vision-app's jar.

**Depends on:** nothing internal (HTTP only) · Angular 21 (`core|common|forms|router|cdk`), `hls.js`, `leaflet` (lazy, and see Gotchas), `rxjs` (WS/preload only) · dev/test: `@angular/cli|build`, `vitest`, `jsdom`
**Used by:** vision-app (packages `dist/` into `META-INF/resources`; vision-api ships no static resources)
**Build/test:** `./mvnw -B -pl station/vision-web clean install` · `-DskipWeb=true` skips Node+Angular entirely · tests `npm run test:ci` (**never** raw `npx vitest`, see Gotchas) · dev `npm start` (proxies `/api`, `/actuator`, `/legacy`, `/hls` → `:8080`)

## API surface

### `core/api/` — the only place wire shapes live
- `models.ts` (~3000 lines) — every DTO, mirroring Java 1:1. Change a Java DTO → fix here first, then `grep -rn` the field.
- `VisionApi` (~123 methods) — thin `firstValueFrom` wrappers over `HttpClient`; no component calls `fetch`/`HttpClient` directly.
- `api-error.ts#describeHttpError(err)` — the one place an HTTP failure becomes user text.
- **`ControlProfile` gained required `stickMode`/`forwardIsUp`; `UpdateControlProfileRequest` gained
  the same pair as optional** (docs/plans/active/CONTROLLER-SETUP-CONTEXT.md wave C15, reconciled here
  as part of merging `feat/controller-setup-c15`) — the owner's transmitter arrangement (mode 1-4,
  which way a vertical axis reads), stored per layout rather than per browser. An older/omitting
  caller of `UpdateControlProfileRequest` gets the platform default read back on the next fetch. See
  `TransmitterView`/`WizardStep` below for where the setup UI reads/writes this.
- **`BatteryThresholds`/`OpsThresholdsResponse` (S3, docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md §2)** — the
  wire mirror of `GET /api/ops/thresholds`, `VisionApi#opsThresholds()`. **`DiscoveryInboxResponse` now
  wraps `DiscoveryCandidate[]` in `{candidates, sources}`; `DiscoverySource` (`{id, status: 'OK' |
  'UNREACHABLE'}`) is new (A3, same plan §2)** — `listDiscoveryInboxCandidates()`'s return type changed
  accordingly; see `core/ops/`/`core/discovery/` below for the consumers. `AssetUsage`/`UsageSummary`
  both gained an optional `pilotId` mirroring the backend's own addition (no frontend consumer yet).
- **`FollowState`/`FollowStatus` (new) + `StreamTracksResponse.follow?: FollowStatus`** (docs/plans/active/
  TRACK-FOLLOW-PLAN.md §3.1, wave W4) — the follow lock's own lifecycle, orthogonal to the sibling
  `lockedTrackId` sentinel on the same response (which reads `0` in `LOST` while `follow.trackId`
  stays bound). `FollowState = 'REQUESTING'|'HOLDING'|'COASTING'|'LOST'|'RELEASED'`. Omitted entirely
  (not `null`) when no lock was ever issued or after one `RELEASED` read. See `shared/player/`'s
  `follow-hud/` bullet below for the one reader.
- **`SeatKind`/`SeatHolderResponse`/`SeatsResponse`/`TakeSeatRequest` (new)** (docs/plans/active/
  CREW-CONTROL-PLAN.md §3.6, wave W3) — `GET /api/assets/{id}/seats`/`POST .../seats/{kind}`'s wire
  shape, byte-exact: explicit-null holder fields (`holderUserId`/`holderDisplayName`/`acquiredAt`/
  `expiresAt`, all `null` on a free seat, `mine` included) and three `may*` authority booleans that
  are purely server-derived — the frontend never re-derives authority from role/capability. `{kind}`
  is `flight`|`camera`, lowercase exactly (`VisionApi#takeAssetSeat`/`releaseAssetSeat` lowercase the
  `SeatKind` union on the way out). See `core/seat/`/`features/crew/` below for the one consumer.

### `core/**` — stores (all `providedIn: 'root'` unless noted)
| Area | Types |
|---|---|
| fleet | `FleetStore` (devices+streams, 5s poll; `run()` is the toast boundary) |
| live | `LiveStore` — one SSE connection, topic subscribe/unsubscribe, append-only log w/ per-consumer cursors |
| telemetry / detections | `TelemetryStore`, `DetectionsStore` (`DETECTIONS_LIMIT=50` retained batches; **`followTracks(streamId, wanted: boolean)`**, docs/plans/active/TRACK-FOLLOW-PLAN.md §3.5, wave W4 — a thin `wanted`-boolean wrapper over `trackTracks`/`untrackTracks`, now driven by `CockpitFacade`'s own `wantsTracksPoll`-gated effect rather than by `CvControlPanel`'s mounted lifetime, so the `GET .../tracks` poll — and with it, the only way to observe a `LOST→HOLDING` recovery — survives a closed Vision drawer; see the `fly/` bullet below for D1). `telemetry-logic.ts#freshness(age)` → `'live'\|'aging'\|'stale'\|'none'` (wave H1, docs/plans/active/OPERATOR-UX-3-PLAN.md finding H1) is now the one source of truth for "is this sample too old to act on", a thin relabel of the pre-existing `telemetryAgeSeverity` tiers (`fresh→live`, `amber→aging`, `red→stale`, `undefined→none`) — callers that need a UI-facing tri-state read this, not `telemetryAgeSeverity` directly. `humanAge(seconds)` → `12s` / `3m 10s` / `4h 2m` / `4d 2h` (always both units of its tier, including a zero remainder) is the one duration formatter for telemetry age across the OSD, the Controller drawer, and the cockpit's not-streaming card. |
| map-data | `LayersStore`, `MarksStore`, `DrawingsStore`, `TracksStore` — **all four are ref-counted `activate()`/`release()` since ALWAYS-ON-FLOW wave C3 (2026-09-06): the initial `GET` *and* the 30s poll both live under `activate()`, so nothing fetches until a consumer is mounted and nothing polls after the last one leaves. Every direct injector — a routed facade *or* a presentational child under `shared/map/map-controls/**` — must `activate()` in its constructor and `release()` from its own `DestroyRef.onDestroy`.** **Also gated on live itself since LIVE-POLL-RETIREMENT waves L1a/L1b/L1c (docs/plans/active/LIVE-POLL-RETIREMENT-PLAN.md §3 D1, 2026-09-06): each store's own `private applyTransport(liveAvailable: boolean)` composes `activeConsumers`-demand with `isLiveAvailable(LiveStore.connectionState())` — the 30s poll now runs only while a consumer is active *and* live is unavailable; a genuine transition into live does exactly one reconcile `GET`, a transition out immediately re-fetches once then resumes polling. `applyTransport` is copied into each store (not a shared helper — deliberate, keeps wave file scopes disjoint), each carrying a private `liveGated: boolean` field that starts `false` so the very first transport call is never treated as a spurious no-op (the boot-time double-fetch this guards against). **L1c, `LayersStore` only:** a layer arriving over the `map` SSE topic never carries `grants` (§4.3) — `applyLayerEvents` preserves the previously-known list, correct for everything except a grant **revocation**, which that fold cannot represent. `LayersStore` compensates with `scheduleGrantsReconcile()`, a raw-`setTimeout` ~1s debounce (not `PollScheduler` — matches `toast.service.ts`'s own precedent) fired off any `layer`-entity live delta, independent of `activeConsumers`.** + `layers-logic`/`mark-logic`/`drawings-logic`/`map-event-logic`. **`RouteStore` (new, docs/plans/active/COMMAND-MAP-FLOW-PLAN.md §3.4, wave W3)** is page-provided, **not** `providedIn: 'root'` — an on-demand two-hop fetch (`listUsages` → `usageTimeline`, never `.../telemetry`), same posture as `FleetMapStore`/`WeatherStore` below, not the always-on/live-folding shape every other row in this line has; `route-logic.ts` (pure: `AssetRoute`/`RouteSpan`/`buildAssetRoute`/`routeUsageLimit`) |
| map / geofence / weather | `FleetMapStore`, `GeofenceStore` (**ref-counted `activate()`/`release()`, wave C3 — same contract as the `map-data` row above**), `WeatherStore` |
| identity | `AuthStore`, `OrgStore`, `SettingsStore` |
| geo | `GeoStore` (visual-geo correction), `camera-geo/` (fixed-camera pose, pure) |
| seat | **`SeatStore` (new, docs/plans/active/CREW-CONTROL-PLAN.md §3.6, wave W3)** — page-provided, not `providedIn:'root'`; `seat-logic.ts` (pure: `singleOperatorSeats`, `seatFor`, `renewalIntervalMs`). See the `features/` → `crew/`/`core/seat/` bullets below for the full renew-while-mine cadence. |
| rc | `RcInputService`, `VirtualRcInputService`, `KeyboardRcInputService`, `RcSource`, `ManualControlClient`, `ControlActionDispatcher`, `ControlProfileStore`; `keyboard-action-logic.ts` (pure, MAVLINK-COMMANDS-PLAN.md D3) — action-key chord parsing (`actionKeyIdFor`), catalogue-driven resolution (`resolveActionKey`), live-armed-state verb (`toggleArmVerb`), edge detection (`newActionKeyPresses`), hold-cancellation (`holdContinues`), and the shared `isTypingTarget` guard |
| events | `EventsStore` (`GET /api/events`), `SystemEventsStore` (`core/system-events/system-events-logic.ts` — maps the generic `LiveEvent`/`event` SSE feed to bell rows + severities; **S4, docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md §2**: `SEVERITY_BY_TYPE`/`TITLE_BY_TYPE` gained `LINK_LOST`/`BATTERY_LOW`, both `danger`, feeding `NotificationBell` — nine event kinds now render with a real label/icon/severity instead of falling through to any generic default), `TracksStore`, `TrainingStore`, `SystemStatusStore` |
| ops | **`ThresholdsStore` (new, S3, docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md §2)** — `core/ops/thresholds-store.ts`, `providedIn: 'root'`, fetch-once on construction (no `PollScheduler` — served battery thresholds change rarely enough that a page refresh is an acceptable staleness bound, unlike the 5-30s pollers elsewhere in this table). `GET /api/ops/thresholds` → `battery: BatteryThresholds`; a failed fetch leaves the signal at `core/ops/thresholds-logic.ts#DEFAULT_BATTERY_THRESHOLDS` (`{warningPercent: 25, criticalPercent: 10}`, frozen) rather than blocking or fabricating a value — CLAUDE.md's degrade-honestly rule applied to config, not just data. **One severity source**: `core/fleet/attention-logic.ts#batteryAttentionSeverity`/`attentionReasons` and `core/telemetry/telemetry-logic.ts#batterySeverity` both now take an optional `thresholds: BatteryThresholds` parameter (defaulting to `DEFAULT_BATTERY_THRESHOLDS` — every existing call site compiles unchanged), and `features/fly/fly-osd.ts` is the one live consumer wiring `ThresholdsStore.battery()` through to `batterySeverity`, replacing the OSD's own previously-hardcoded 20/45 pair. **Boundaries are inclusive (`<=`)** on both functions, matching the backend's own `BatteryThresholdsResponse`/`EventType#BATTERY_LOW` javadoc ("at/below") — `attention-logic.ts` used to be strict-`<`, now unified. **Residual, named honestly**: Command (`AssetPanel`/`CommandFacade`) and Inventory's own `attention-logic.ts` consumers are not yet wired to `ThresholdsStore` — they still resolve through the default-parameter fallback (functionally identical to their pre-S3 hardcoded 20/10, now just centrally defined instead of locally duplicated), not a live served value. Wiring them is a small follow-up (inject `ThresholdsStore`, thread `.battery()` through the existing calls) intentionally left out of this wave's file scope. |
| discovery | `DiscoveryInboxStore` (docs/plans/active/ZERO-CONFIG-ONBOARDING-CONTEXT.md §3 P2/§11, wave Z2d) — `GET /api/discovery/inbox` on a 30s `PollScheduler` cadence, refcounted `activate()`/`release()` (poll runs only while `<vision-found-devices>` is mounted); `discovery-inbox-logic.ts` (pure: method labels, `humanAge`-based age text, NEW/REGISTERED/DISMISSED visibility+sort, per-candidate action availability, register/attach request builders). **A3 (docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md §2)**: the store's `sources` signal now mirrors the response envelope's `sources: DiscoverySource[]`; `discovery-inbox-logic.ts#sourceUnreachableWarnings(sources)` turns each `UNREACHABLE` source into "`<Method>` unreachable — found devices may be incomplete." (reusing `discoveryMethodLabel`) — see the `inventory/` → `<vision-found-devices>` bullet below for how that renders. **Also gated on live itself since LIVE-POLL-RETIREMENT wave L2a (docs/plans/active/LIVE-POLL-RETIREMENT-PLAN.md §3 D1/§5 L2, 2026-09-06)** — identical `applyTransport`/`liveGated` shape to the `map-data` row above, this store's own pre-existing `=== 1`/`stopPollingFn` naming left as-is rather than churned to match the map stores' `> 1`/`stopPollFn`. **The L2b gotcha**: the `discovery` SSE topic (`LiveStore.discoveryEvents()`) carries only candidate deltas, never `sources` — so once live is up and the 30s poll has stopped, `sources` can only ever change on the one reconcile `GET` a genuine reconnect (`poll → live` transition) triggers; it is not a nicety, it is the only channel `sources` has left. `discovery-inbox-store.spec.ts` (new — this store's first spec) asserts this explicitly. |
| shell/ui | `SidebarStore`, `ThemeStore`, `UiStore` (exclusive overlays), `GlobalOverlayStore`, `ToastService`, `PollScheduler`, `IdlePreload`, `LeafletWarmup`, `panel-state.ts` |
| pure-logic only (no store) | `audit/` (+ `audit/summary-logic.ts` — `humanizeSummary`/`actorLabel`/`buildNameMap`, shared with `features/activity/`), `after-action/`, `readiness/` (`readiness-logic.ts` gained a "Maintenance grounding" section, S1/wave WB1 — `parseGroundingBlocker`/`groundingBlocker`/`groundedBannerText`/`featureBlockers`, the one parser every grounded surface — cockpit banner, Arm gate, both readiness pages — renders from), `roster/`, `activity/`, `command/`, `maintenance/` (`core/maintenance/maintenance-logic.ts`), `stream-info-logic.ts` |

### Routes (one lazy `loadComponent` chunk each; per-feature `*.routes.ts`)
`/fly` picker · `/fly/:assetId` cockpit · `/crew/:assetId` (docs/plans/active/CREW-CONTROL-PLAN.md §3.4, wave W3 — the crew seat) · `/command` · `/wall` · `/live/:deviceId` · `/assets` (now **Inventory**, `?tab=vehicles|equipment|links|categories`, default `vehicles`, wave W4 — see the `inventory/` bullet below) · `/assets/:assetId` · `/assets/:assetId/readiness` · `/assets/:assetId/replay/:usageId` · `/replay` · `/add-source` · `/provision-wifi` (ZERO-CONFIG-ONBOARDING-CONTEXT.md §4/§8, wave Z5 — `orgGuard`, Improv Wi-Fi provisioning over Web Serial) · `/fleet/maintenance` · `/activity` · `/login` · `/org` (guard-only redirect → `/manage/roster?tab=org`, wave W7) · `/settings` · `/vision/profiles` (CV-SETTINGS-PLAN.md §4, wave W6 — `orgGuard`, replaces `/settings/detection`) · `/debug`
Hubs: `/operate`(+`/preflight`, `/missions`) · `/monitor`(+`/alerts`, `/audit`, `/layouts`) · `/manage`(+`/roster` (now **Crew**, `?tab=roster|org`, wave W7), `/controller`, `/system`, `/firmware`, `/geo/regions`, `/training`, `/training/models`, `/training/:datasetId[/samples/:sampleId]`, `/training/jobs/:jobId`, `/training/runs`, `/training/runs/:runId` (wave W8))
Redirects: `/crew` → `/wall` (wave W3 — no crew-specific landing/picker page exists yet; see the `crew/` bullet below). `/map` → `/command`, `/warehouse` → `/assets` (both pages deleted; the stub keeps old bookmarks off the 404). `/org` → `/manage/roster?tab=org` (wave W7 — see the `roster/, org-settings/` bullet below). `/devices` → `/assets?tab=links`, `/manage/categories` → `/assets?tab=categories`, `/manage/reports` → `/assets` (wave W4 — all three folded into the Inventory page's own tabs, `DevicesPage`/`CategoriesPage` themselves unmoved and unchanged; each a `RedirectFunction`, not a plain string, so a `?tab=` param survives the hop — see the `inventory/` bullet below). `/manage/health` → `/fleet/maintenance` (wave W4, closing W7's own exit criterion). `/settings/detection` → `/vision/profiles` (plain-string `redirectTo`+`pathMatch:'full'`, `settings.routes.ts` — CV-SETTINGS-PLAN.md wave W6, `detection-settings.*` deleted outright).

### `features/**`
- **fly/** — `DronePickerPage` + `CockpitPage`/`CockpitFacade`; `flight-command-panel`, `cv-control-panel` (live per-stream CV) + `cv-setup-modal`, `target-list` (`<vision-target-list>`, docs/plans/active/TRACK-FOLLOW-PLAN.md §3.4, wave W5 below — "the second door" onto Follow, mounted inside `cv-control-panel.html`), `fly-hud` (the on-video control HUD, wave WEB1 below — now the sole host of the RC provider stack), `take-control-modal` (the "connect ritual", wave FLY-FLOW W4 below — mounted inside `fly-hud`, not `cockpit.ts`), `rc-monitor` (informational-only as of WEB1), `arm-confirm-dialog`, `mode-picker`, `fly-osd`, `failsafe-banner`, `geo-chip`, `diagnostics-card`. (`marks-panel` moved to `shared/map/map-controls/marks-panel/` in wave W2 of docs/plans/active/COMMAND-MAP-FLOW-PLAN.md — see the `shared/` → `map/` bullet below; the cockpit's `marks`/`map` tool-rail buttons now open the shared `<vision-map-tools>` drawer instead.) Pure: `fly-logic`, `stream-state-logic`, `cv-control-panel-logic`, `flight-command-panel-logic`, `rc-monitor-logic`, `target-list-logic` (wave W5 below); `core/rc/fly-hud-logic.ts` + `core/rc/neutral-gate-logic.ts` (wave WEB1 below).
  - **Target list (docs/plans/active/TRACK-FOLLOW-PLAN.md §3.4, wave W5).** `<vision-target-list>` — inputs `tracks: readonly StreamTrack[]` (required), `followedTrackId: number` (default `0`); output `trackSelected: number`. Injects nothing — a pure dumb component, same "host owns the write" rule as `<vision-follow-hud>`. Pure logic in `target-list-logic.ts#targetRows(tracks, followedTrackId, nowMs): readonly TargetRow[]` — the followed track (if any) always sorts first, the rest by `lastSeen` descending; `age` via `core/telemetry/telemetry-logic.ts#humanAge`/`ageSeconds`; `stateTone`/`stateLabel` map `TrackState` to the table's dot+text (frontend-style §5: one chip per row max, secondary state is a dot, never a second chip). Selection uses the app's one selection language (frontend-style §4: 2px left inset bar `--color-info` + `--color-info-soft` tint, the `vehicles-table.css` precedent) rather than inventing a new one. Empty state is `<vision-empty title="No tracked objects…">`, never a blank table. `cv-control-panel.ts#selectTrack(trackId)` calls the identical `buildFollowLockPatch()` builder `CockpitFacade`'s own glass-click path already uses (same "two doors, one builder function" precedent as `releaseLock()`/`releaseFollow()`) — both doors can never drift on PATCH shape. The old muted "click a box in the video to lock onto it" hint (D7) is deleted from `cv-control-panel.html`: the list makes the gesture discoverable by existing.
  - **Follow-lock glass + a closed drawer no longer un-follows the target (docs/plans/active/TRACK-FOLLOW-PLAN.md, wave W4, defect D1).** `CockpitFacade#lockedTrackId` is now `computed(() => detections.results()[0]?.tracking?.lockedTrackId ?? 0)` — the **per-frame** detections feed, tracked for the whole stream session — instead of an echo of `CvControlPanel`'s own drawer-scoped tracks poll (`lockedTrackIdChange`, **deleted**, including its one call site in `cockpit.html`); the old plumbing zeroed the overlay's lock the instant the Vision drawer closed even though the server-side lock was untouched. New facade reads: `follow: FollowStatus | null` (mirrors `DetectionsStore.tracks()?.follow`), `lostBox: BoundingBox | null` (`follow.lastBox` while `state==='LOST'`, else `null` — fed to `<vision-player>`'s new `lostBox` input), a private `wantsTracksPoll` gate driving `DetectionsStore#followTracks` from a constructor effect (**deviation**: the plan's own formula ORs in "the Vision drawer is open"; that state lives in `cockpit.ts`'s host-owned, deliberately non-injectable `UiStore`, out of this wave's file scope and unreachable via DI — `detectionOn()` substitutes, since it fails toward *more* polling, never toward silently missing a `LOST` recovery window; a later wave touching `cockpit.ts` should wire the real boolean through instead), and `releaseFollow()`/`reacquireFollow()` (reusing `buildReleaseLockPatch()`/`followTrack()`). `CvControlPanel` keeps its own "Following #N" chip (still reads the slower tracks-poll copy — fine, since it only renders while mounted anyway) but no longer starts/stops the poll itself. New `<vision-follow-hud>` mounted first-child inside `cockpit.html`'s `.main-left` (its `column-reverse` layout puts DOM-first at the visual bottom): `[follow]="facade.follow()"`, `[canRelease]="!facade.watchMode()"`, `(release)="facade.releaseFollow()"`, `(reacquire)="facade.reacquireFollow()"` — see `shared/`'s `follow-hud/` bullet for the component itself, and `shared/`'s `player/` bullet for the `lostBox` draw pass on `<vision-player>`.
  - **CV settings dual-write stopped; one "From profile" line + explicit save (docs/plans/active/CV-SETTINGS-PLAN.md, wave W7).** Every fly-time CV control (`cv-control-panel.ts`, `cv-setup-modal.ts`, `shared/player/detections-strip.ts`) now renders from a `ResolvedCvConfig` — `CockpitFacade#resolvedCvConfig`, folding `effectiveProfile` (`GET /api/cv/profiles/effective?assetId=`) and `streamConfig` (`GET /api/streams/{id}/config`, H6 — the tracking readback the wire previously had none for) into one shape — never from `SettingsStore` (its CV-defaults slice, `model`/`confidenceThreshold`/`inferenceFps`/`labelFilter`/`labelDenyFilter`/`detectionEnabled`/`tracking`/`effective()`/`adjust()`, is **deleted outright**; `StartStreamRequest` sends no CV body any more — the server resolves the initial config from the profile hierarchy). `cv-control-panel-logic.ts#effectiveProfileLine` renders `From profile "<name>" (<source>)` (or `Platform defaults`) above the "Looking for" summary and in the setup modal's header. A `canManageOrg`-gated **"Save to this asset's profile"** button in `CvSetupModal`'s footer (`canManage` threaded in from `CockpitFacade#canManage`, not re-derived) creates/updates a `CvProfile` from the modal's live config (`buildProfileRequestFromConfig`) then `PUT`s an `ASSET`-scope binding — explicit only, never a side effect of a hot-knob edit; states once, in its own hint, "Applies to this asset's next stream start." `CvSetupModal` carries a component-local `pendingEdits`/`liveConfig` overlay (a `signal<Partial<ResolvedCvConfig>>` merged onto the `config` input, reset whenever a fresh readback arrives) so pre-start edits stage meaningfully into that save action, and so two fields edited inside one `debounce()` window can't silently drop the earlier one. `onModelChange` sends **two** sequential PATCHes (model alone, then a hot-knob-shaped PATCH carrying the seeded `labelFilter`) rather than one combined PATCH — `UpdateStreamConfigRequest`'s own doc comment (`core/api/models.ts`) freezes model-change and hot-knob edits as mutually exclusive on the wire, so a drag can never accidentally trigger a re-arm. **H12**: `SettingsStore.declutterLevel` (persisted, `vision.settings.declutterLevel`) replaces three previously-unshared in-memory `boxesMode` signals on `CockpitFacade`/`LiveFacade`/`WallTile` — all three now alias the same store signal, so every wall tile app-wide shares one declutter level by design, labelled "View · Boxes (shortcut: B)" per §3.5. **H8**: `LiveFacade.onConfidence`/`onFps`/`onModel` deleted (grep-confirmed zero template bindings first — the unused `SettingsStore`-writing duplicates the plan named). See `docs/plans/active/CV-SETTINGS-CONTEXT.md`'s "W7 → W8" handoff for the full deviation list and bundle delta.
  - **Stale telemetry never impersonates live (wave H1, docs/plans/active/OPERATOR-UX-3-PLAN.md finding H1, §2 H1)** — CLAUDE.md rule 9 ("newest data … even if previous is still available") cuts both ways: a stale sample can still be shown, but never *as if* it were current. `TelemetryStore#track()` follows a device's owning asset's currently-open usage (`endedAt === undefined`); an unclosed session (streaming stopped without a clean end) leaves old samples reachable long after `CockpitFacade#live()` goes false — the exact shape of H1's own finding (a rover with a 4-day-old sample, live confirmed on the running dev server).
    - `fly-osd.ts`/`.html`/`.css` — new `fly-osd-logic.ts` (pure, `isStaleReading`/`osdGroupLabel`/`armedOsdText`) drives three changes while `freshness(sampleAgeSeconds) === 'stale'`: the Power and Nav metric groups dim to `--text-muted` (numbers stay, never blanked — CLAUDE.md "degrade honestly", the value is still the truest one available) and their structural-register label switches from `POWER`/`NAV` to `LAST KNOWN · <humanAge>` (both groups render the identical string — they share one sample's age, no separate per-group identity to preserve); `armedText` reads `ARMED?` rather than a confident `ARMED` (never for `DISARMED`, which is not a confidence claim). `ageLabel` now formats through `humanAge` instead of a bare `Ns`. Link/Env groups are untouched — the age chip itself already carries the existing red severity tier.
    - `rc-monitor-logic.ts#armedChip` gained a second `ageSeconds` parameter: stale flips it to a neutral (never `ok`-toned) chip reading `Armed 4d ago`/`Disarmed 4d ago` instead of bare `ARMED`/`DISARMED`. New `sampleIsStale(ageSeconds)` drives the mode chip's own fade (`.state-strip .chip.stale { color: var(--text-faint) }`, `rc-monitor.css`). `rc-monitor.ts` gained a `sampleAgeSeconds` input — `cockpit.html` threads it in from `facade.telemetry.sampleAgeSeconds()` (the one new binding on `<vision-rc-monitor>`); no other prop of the drawer changed (transmitter view, command rows, footer are all wave H1 out-of-scope, untouched).
    - **Cockpit not-streaming card** (`cockpit.html`/`.css`, `cockpit-facade.ts#notStreamingLastSeen`/`notStreamingPosition`) — `vision-player` itself still renders its own bare "Not streaming"/"Stream stopped" caption (`player.html`, out of scope for this wave); a new centred `.not-streaming-card` (opaque `--panel-raised`, not this cockpit's usual `--hud-*` frosted chrome — the frame behind it is a static idle/black one, not unpredictable video, so the HUD-over-imagery rule doesn't apply) is a later DOM sibling within `.stage-video` and renders on top of it whenever `facade.primaryDevice() && !facade.live()`. Shows "Not streaming", `Last seen 4d 2h ago` (or `Never seen` — `notStreamingLastSeen` degrades honestly when no sample has ever arrived), the last position as `.mono` (`positionLabel`, reused verbatim from `fly-logic.ts` — same live-fix-else-last-known fallback `weatherPosition`/`dronePosition` already use) with an `Open on map ›` `routerLink="/command"` only when a position exists, and reuses the exact bottom-control `facade.start()` call (gated `!facade.watchMode()` — a viewer never sees it) rather than inventing a second "start stream" path.
  - **`DronePickerPage`/`DronePickerFacade` (wave T1, docs/plans/active/OPERATOR-UX-3-PLAN.md finding T1, §2 T1)** — the `/fly` grid is now two groups, **Your vehicles** / **Simulated** (`facade.groups(): PickerGroups`, `drone-picker-logic.ts#groupAndSort`), each independently sorted streaming-first then `lastUsedAt` descending, never-seen last (`sortByFreshness`, distinct from `fly-logic.ts#sortAssetsForPicker` — that one stays a flat list, still backing `cockpit-facade.ts#orderedSwitcherAssets`, the cockpit header's own drone switcher, untouched by this wave). A **Hide simulated** checkbox (`facade.hideSimulated`, persisted at `vision.fly.hideSimulated` via `core/panel-state.ts#readPersistedFlag`/`writePersistedFlag` — the same idiom `live-facade.ts`/`cockpit-facade.ts` already use) sits in the page bar's `[pageBarFilters]` slot and collapses just the Simulated group's card grid; its header (with count) stays visible. `isSimulated`/`SIMULATED_CATEGORY` mirror `features/devices/simulate-logic.ts#isSimulatedAsset`/`SIMULATED_CATEGORY` (duplicated, not imported — no cross-feature `*-logic.ts` import precedent exists in this codebase; `AssetSummary`, the picker's own `listAssets()` shape, still carries no per-device signal today, so category is the only fact either place can read — `SOURCE-ONBOARDING-CONTEXT.md` §4 tracks moving this to a device-level fact). Every per-card derivation (status label, last-seen, position) moved off the page and onto the new **`DronePickerCard`** (`<vision-drone-picker-card>`, `[asset]` input) so the same markup renders under both groups without duplicating it or reaching for `NgTemplateOutlet` context typing. An offline card's chip now reads its honest age instead of the bare word "Offline" — `drone-picker-logic.ts#offlineLabel` → `Offline · 2h 29m` / `Offline · 6d 4h` / `Never seen` (via `core/telemetry/telemetry-logic.ts#humanAge`, the app's one age vocabulary); a streaming card is unchanged (bare "Streaming" + the live dot).
    - **`groupAndSort`/`isSimulated`/`SIMULATED_CATEGORY`/`offlineLabel`/`PickerGroups` moved to `core/fleet/triage-logic.ts` (docs/plans/active/OPERATOR-UX-4-PLAN.md finding N3, §2 N3, wave W2)** when `CommandFacade#railGroups` needed the identical triage — `drone-picker-logic.ts` is now a pure re-export shim so every pre-existing import site (`drone-picker.ts`, `drone-picker-card.ts`, `drone-picker-facade.ts`) keeps working verbatim; `/fly`'s own behavior is unchanged byte-for-byte (its own spec, moved verbatim to `core/fleet/triage-logic.spec.ts`, still passes). `groupAndSort`/`PickerGroups` gained a generic `<T extends TriageCandidate>` (the four fields the sort logic actually reads — `displayName`/`category`/`status`/`lastUsedAt`) plus an optional `attentionRank` argument, defaulting to `AssetSummary` so `/fly`'s own untyped call sites are unaffected; `CommandFacade#railGroups` instantiates it with a synthesized `RailRow` (arithmetic-derived from `AssetAttention`'s differently-shaped fields — `lastUsedAt` recovered from `telemetryAgeMs`, never fabricated) and its own `rowRank` as `attentionRank`.
    - **"My assigned" section (B4, docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md §2)** — `drone-picker-logic.ts` now genuinely owns logic again, not purely a re-export shim: `myAssignedAssets(assets, assignedAssetIds)` filters `facade.groups().yours` down to the ids `GET /api/me/assignments` (`VisionApi#myAssignments()`, wired for the first time — the method existed with zero callers) returned. `DronePickerFacade#refreshAssignments()` fetches once on construction (not `PollScheduler`-backed — assignments change on a manager's own timescale, not worth polling) and degrades to an empty set on failure (`console.warn`, same as the store's own poll-failure idiom), which folds `myAssigned()` back to `[]` — the picker falls back to exactly today's flat groups, never a blocked page. Deliberately **additive, not partitioning**: an assigned asset still also appears in "Your vehicles" below (duplication, not a filter) — kept local to this file rather than moved into the shared `core/fleet/triage-logic.ts#PickerGroups`/`groupAndSort` (also consumed by `CommandFacade#railGroups`, which has no per-pilot assignment concept and shouldn't gain a phantom empty section for it). `drone-picker.html` renders a new "My assigned (n)" `.picker-group` above "Your vehicles", `@if`-gated to only appear once the list is non-empty.
    - **Replay an earlier flight (D3r, docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md §2)** — the cockpit's "Replay last flight" link (`cockpit.html`, next to the flight controls) gained a `<vision-kebab-menu>` beside it, populated from `CockpitFacade#olderReplayableUsages` (`fly-logic.ts#earlierReplayableUsages(usages, max = REPLAY_PICKER_MAX_USAGES)`, `MAX = 5`) — `AssetDetails#recentUsages` is already a capped-to-20, newest-first list the facade polls every ~5s, so this needed **no new endpoint**: the function drops the newest (already the "last flight" link's own target) and keeps only finished usages (`endedAt !== undefined`, an open/in-progress usage is never offered as a replay). Renders as one `.mono` ISO timestamp per menu row (`asset-detail.html`'s own "more actions" kebab is the precedent for this exact button-list-inside-`<details>` idiom, reused rather than hand-building new popover CSS), each linking to `/assets/:assetId/replay/:usageId`. The menu itself is omitted entirely when there are no older finished usages — no empty "Replay an earlier flight (0)" affordance.
    - **Grounded is visible in the cockpit (S1, docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md §2 "S1 gate semantics", wave WB1)** — before this wave, nothing in `features/fly/**` said a word about custody grounding or an open flight-blocking `MaintenanceRecord`; the pilot just hit Arm and ate a server-side `409 REFUSED:maintenance-grounded`. `core/readiness/readiness-logic.ts` gained the shared parser every grounded surface now renders from: `MAINTENANCE_BLOCKER_PREFIX = 'MAINTENANCE_GROUNDED:'`, `parseGroundingBlocker(raw)` → `{kind: MaintenanceKind, summary}` or `null`, `groundingBlocker(blockers)` (first match), `groundedBannerText(blocker)` → `"<MAINTENANCE_KIND_LABELS[kind]> — <summary>"` (e.g. `"Grounded — Prop strike on landing"`), and `featureBlockers(blockers)` (every *non*-grounding entry, for callers that must not also render the grounding string as an opaque feature row — see the `readiness-facade.ts` fix below). New **`GroundingStore`** (`features/fly/grounding-store.ts`, `@Injectable()`, component-provided, not `providedIn: 'root'`) owns the one `GET /api/assets/{id}/readiness` fetch per selected asset and exposes `groundedReason: Signal<string | undefined>`. **Deliberately its own class, not folded into `CockpitFacade`'s own body** — `core/telemetry/preflight-readiness-independence.spec.ts` (drone-onboarding wave O6) literal-scans `cockpit-facade.ts` for 8 readiness-API tokens (`ReadinessReport`, `assetReadiness`, etc., protecting the live-telemetry preflight checklist's total independence from the readiness API) and `GroundingStore`/`groundedReason` match none of them. `CockpitFacade` injects `GroundingStore` (same page-provided injector as `GeoStore`, listed alongside it in `CockpitPage`'s own `providers:` array) and drives `track()`/`reset()` from its own `activeAssetId`-keyed `effect()`, byte-for-byte mirroring the file's own pre-existing `geo.track(assetId)`/`geo.reset()` pattern — exposing one plain `readonly groundedReason = computed(() => this.grounding.groundedReason())` pass-through; `CockpitPage` itself injects only `CockpitFacade` (`core/ui/architecture.spec.ts`'s own "injects no `*Store` directly" guard). New **`GroundedBanner`** (`<vision-grounded-banner [reason]>`, `grounded-banner.ts/.html/.css`) renders in the same CSS-grid `banner` area as `FailsafeBanner` (`cockpit.css`'s `.grid-banner`, now `display:flex;flex-direction:column` so a grounded vehicle mid-flight can show both banners stacked rather than overlapping) — `--color-danger`, shield-iconed (a custody fact, not a flight-state one). `<vision-flight-command-panel>` gained a `groundedReason` input (forwarded through `<vision-rc-monitor>`) driving `armDisabled = computed(() => groundedReason() !== undefined)`: the Arm button is `[disabled]="anyBusy() || armDisabled()"` with `[title]="groundedReason() ?? 'Arm — the propellers will spin'"` and an inline `.disabled-reason` line rendering the reason verbatim (the codebase's existing disabled-with-reason idiom, first established by `rc-monitor-logic.ts#engageBlock`) — `requestArm()` guard-returns while grounded, so a stray Enter on the disabled DOM button can't reach the server. **Disarm/mode/RTL stay completely untouched** — S1's frozen rule: recovery verbs must always work on a vehicle that is somehow already moving.
    - **B2 — kill navigational dead ends (docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md §2).** `cockpit.html`'s header gained a shield icon-button, `routerLink="['/assets', assetId, 'readiness']"`, next to the map toggle. `/assets/:assetId/readiness` gained the return trip, `Open in Fly ›` in its `pageBarActions` slot (see the `readiness/` bullet below) — the "Asset" crumb already covered readiness → asset-detail, so this closes the one remaining direction.
  - **`rc-monitor`** (the Fly "Controller" drawer) — rebuilt in wave X2 around `vision-transmitter-view` (see `ui/` below). Top to bottom: a **state strip** of plain `.chip`s (armed/disarmed from `armed()`, faint `—` when `undefined`; current `mode()` chip only when the parent supplies one — `CockpitPage` wires it from `facade.telemetry.latest()?.flightState?.mode`, omitted entirely when absent rather than faked; a connected-gamepad chip with device label + live update-rate Hz) with the existing gamepad-unsupported/USB-mode/watchdog `vision-notice`s underneath, only when true; the `TransmitterView` mirror (`interactive` only when engaged *and* the source is `virtual`, wired to `VirtualRcInputService`; prefers the engaged frame's own `client.channelMap()`, falls back to the active profile's `channelMap` before engage) plus a "Centre all" button shown only while interactive; `vision-flight-command-panel` below it with a computed "also on `<switch>`" hint per row (`rc-monitor-logic.ts#modeAlsoOnHint`/`armAlsoOnHint`, derived from the active profile's `actionMap` — the label of whichever switch fires `SET_MODE`/`ARM`|`TOGGLE_ARM`, "↑" suffix when arm is bound to the switch's `HIGH` position). The old axis-bar list, switch pills, "Bound switches" and "Channel map" text lists are gone, replaced by the one `TransmitterView`. The input-source segmented control + engage/release button moved into `SidePanel`'s `[footer]` slot (its first real consumer — see `ui/` `SidePanel` below): engaged renders a big danger `RELEASE` button plus link chips (rate Hz, RTT, profile name) instead of the old "Live — …" prose notice; denied/released/idle states are unchanged (`vision-notice` + `Take control`/`Try again` + `disabled-reason` text).
    - **Wave K (CONTROLLER-UX-PLAN.md §5) — keyboard input source.** `core/rc/keyboard-rc-input.service.ts` (`KeyboardRcInputService`, per-host like its two siblings) is a third `RcSourceKind` (`'gamepad'|'virtual'|'keyboard'`, `core/rc/rc-source.service.ts`): keys drive *functions*, not axis indexes — `W`/`S` (+`↑`/`↓` on a one-pad layout) throttle, `A`/`D` whichever of yaw/steering the vehicle binds, and on a two-pad layout the freed-up arrows become `↑`/`↓` pitch and `←`/`→` roll — read off whatever `channelMap` `rc-monitor.ts#normalizedChannelMap` currently holds (the engaged frame, or the active profile before one exists — bound continuously, not just from `engaged`, so a tap already moves the transmitter picture before Take control the same as decision U1). Values ramp linearly over `RAMP_MS=250ms` (a tap nudges, a hold reaches full deflection); on release a centred control springs back (`control-surface-logic.ts#springsBack`) while a unidirectional one holds; two keys for the same function (the one-pad `W`/`↑` redundancy, or opposite keys) fold to one net direction rather than double-ramping or fighting. `setEnabled(false)` (driven by `RcSource` whenever another source is selected), a window `blur`, and `document.hidden` all release every held key immediately — the same instant-cutoff guarantee the gamepad's unplug deadman gives; a keydown is ignored while focus is in an `<input>`/`<select>`/`<textarea>`/contenteditable. `RcSource#keyboard` is injected `{ optional: true }` — see the Gotchas entry below — so the field is `null` in the couple of hand-built test harnesses that never select `'keyboard'`, never in `rc-monitor.ts`'s own real DI graph. The footer's segmented control gained a third **Keyboard** button, appended *after* Transmitter (so existing index-based `.rc-source button` queries in `rc-monitor.spec.ts` stayed valid); selecting it shows a quiet key-legend line under the transmitter picture (`rc-monitor-logic.ts#keyLegendLines`, `.label`'s structural register, built from the same bound controls' own labels — never invented text) **and** the transmitter picture itself grows a second row list, `.tv-keys`, for the action keys (MAVLINK-COMMANDS-PLAN.md D3, wave W2 below — Space/`Shift`+`Enter`/`1`-`4`, gauge+text rows in the exact idiom a bound switch's own row uses, including the hold-to-fire sweep for `Shift`+`Enter` and any dangerous mode digit). The transmitter view stays a mirror for keyboard exactly like a plugged gamepad (`interactive` is still only `engaged && source.kind()==='virtual'`) — there is nothing to drag, the picture already reflects what the held keys are driving.
    - **Wave R (CONTROLLER-UX-PLAN.md §5) — readiness rows under Take control.** `rc-monitor.ts` reads this asset's own `GET /api/assets/{id}/readiness` directly (`VisionApi#assetReadiness`, the same call `readiness-facade.ts` makes — no new endpoint, and deliberately *not* plumbed through `CockpitFacade`/`cockpit.html` since nothing else in the cockpit needs it) into a private signal, `undefined` while loading or on a failed read (CLAUDE.md "degrade honestly" — never a fabricated warning). `rc-monitor-logic.ts#rcReadinessRows(report)` picks out just the `rc-relay` feature (FLEET-RADIO-PLAN.md R6 — the one row that governs whether this vehicle's link actually satisfies what the relay needs) and is `[]` whenever it's `READY`, absent, or the report itself is absent. `#engageBlock(gate, readiness)` decides what renders under the button: `engageDisabledReason`'s existing single-line text when a more fundamental gate blocks (asset/commandability/gamepad — priority order unchanged), else the readiness row(s) once every one of those is clear — **advisory only**, it never touches `engageDisabled`/whether the button itself is clickable. Rendered as a quiet dot+label(+detail) list (no chip-per-row) with an "Open preflight ›" `routerLink` to `/operate/preflight`, via one shared `<ng-template #engageBlockTpl>` + `NgTemplateOutlet` reused across the `@switch (client.state())`'s denied/released/default cases (avoids tripling the markup).
    - **Wave Z1 (docs/plans/active/ZERO-CONFIG-ONBOARDING-CONTEXT.md §3 P4) — `AssetSessionController#engage`/`#disengage` get their first web caller.** Before this wave the Fly cockpit's arm/mode/RC commands silently depended on a video stream having been started first (`UsageTracker#deviceStreamStarted` was the only thing that ever opened an `AssetUsage`), so a telemetry-only asset (a rover with no camera, or one being readied before its camera comes up) had no working path to commandability at all. Placed in `rc-monitor.html` between the existing "These do nothing…" hint and `<vision-flight-command-panel>` — exactly where the old flow rendered nothing (`flight-command-panel.html`'s own `@if (canCommand())` wraps its entire template) — rather than a new page. New `rc-monitor-logic.ts#resolveSessionAffordance(hasTelemetryDevice, live, operatorEngaged)` (+4 spec cases) is the single gating rule: `'none'` with no telemetry device, or whenever a video stream is already `live` (starting the stream already opens the same kind of usage, and `AssetSessionController#disengage`'s own contract only *demotes*, never closes, a usage a running stream still depends on — an "End session" button would be actively misleading there), else `'engage'`/`'end'` off `operatorEngaged`. This one condition also covers item 5's "may additionally appear when video exists but isn't streaming" case for free, without touching `cockpit.html`'s video-stage notices at all. Calm styling throughout (`.btn.secondary`, no `.big`/`.danger`) — deliberately not styled anything like the Take-control footer below it, since this is a reversible act, not a safety control.
      - **No `GET .../session` read endpoint exists** (`AssetSessionController` only ever had the two write verbs) — rather than a local "I clicked it" flag, `CockpitFacade#operatorEngaged` derives the fact honestly off the same `AssetDetails#recentUsages` its existing 5s asset poll already refreshes: `selectOpenUsage(usages)?.origin === 'OPERATOR'` (`core/telemetry/telemetry-logic.ts`, reused, not duplicated). `core/api/models.ts#AssetUsage` widened with `phase?: UsagePhase`/`origin?: UsageOrigin` (new exported unions) to mirror `dto.AssetUsageResponse` 1:1 — both were on the wire already and simply weren't read. `CockpitFacade#engageSession()`/`#endSession()` force an immediate `loadAsset()` re-fetch on success (mirroring no existing precedent exactly, but the same "the write's own response is never the truth" posture) so the chip flips within one round trip instead of waiting up to the full 5s poll period.
      - **`core/api/vision-api.ts`** gained `engageAssetSession(assetId)` (`POST /api/assets/{id}/session` → `AssetUsage`) and `disengageAssetSession(assetId)` (`DELETE …/session` → `void`), documented as fire-and-confirm, not fire-and-forget, for the same read-endpoint-absence reason. `core/fleet/fleet-store.ts` gained `engageAsset`/`disengageAsset`, `run()`-wrapped like every other mutation there but **without** a `refresh()` call — engaging/disengaging touches neither the device nor stream list `FleetStore` tracks, unlike `start`/`stop`.
      - **Naming**: `resolveSessionAffordance`/`SessionAffordance`/`engageAssetLink`/`endAssetSession`/`engageSession`/`endSession`, all deliberately distinct from `rc-monitor.ts`'s pre-existing `engage()`/`release()` (`ManualControlClient`'s unrelated RC take-control verb) — the two live side by side in the same file/component and must not collide.
      - **Role-gating**: none beyond the existing visibility scope `AssetSessionController` itself enforces server-side (read-scope, not `canManage` — an operator act, not an administrative one, per that controller's own javadoc); the affordance is gated purely on `hasTelemetryDevice`/`live`/`operatorEngaged`, the same facts every pilot viewing this drawer already has.
      - **Dev parity**: unaffected — `vision.auth.enabled=false`'s dev admin resolves visibility as unbounded exactly as before, so the affordance behaves identically in dev and prod for a given telemetry/live state.
      - **Degrades honestly**: `operatorEngaged`/`live` are read from the same poll every other cockpit fact already trusts, never a local flag that could drift from what the server actually did; a failed engage/disengage POST/DELETE surfaces through `FleetStore`'s existing `run()` → `describeHttpError` → one toast, same as every other mutation, and the affordance simply stays in its pre-click state (no optimistic flip).
      - **Verify chain, all green**: `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`/`-p tsconfig.spec.json` — 0 errors both. `npm run test:ci` — **162/162 files, 3135/3135 tests** (+14 over the prior 3121 baseline — the 4 new `resolveSessionAffordance` cases plus this wave landing on a tree where the backend Z1 half, `feat(zero-config Z1): engage opens telemetry subscriptions`, was already the branch's HEAD commit). `npx ng build --configuration production` — green, same one pre-existing bundle-budget warning only (390 kB initial budget, already exceeded before this wave). **Bundle delta**, measured via a pathspec-scoped `git stash push -- <this wave's 10 files>` baseline (isolated from `infra/rover-sim/**`/`drone-link/**`'s own pre-existing unrelated uncommitted changes on this tree): initial bundle **420.28 kB → 420.77 kB raw (+0.49 kB), 118.33 kB → 118.38 kB transfer (+0.05 kB)** — the two new eager `VisionApi`/`FleetStore` methods. Lazy **`cockpit` chunk 151.43 kB → 153.82 kB raw (+2.39 kB), 33.06 kB → 33.54 kB transfer (+0.48 kB)** — the new `rc-monitor` inputs/outputs/computed/template/CSS.
  - **Wave WEB1 (docs/plans/active/FLY-CONTROL-UX-PLAN.md — the on-video control HUD + neutral-stick arm gate, reversing CONTROLLER-UX U5's "everything in the drawer" call).** §0's own framing: "chips, bars and pills move onto the video; every *sentence* leaves it." New **`fly-hud`** (`<vision-fly-hud>`, `features/fly/fly-hud.{ts,html,css}`) mounts unconditionally in `cockpit.html` (once an asset is selected, replacing the old `@if (isPanelOpen('rc'))`-gated `<vision-rc-monitor>` mount) and is now **the sole owner of the RC provider stack** — `RcInputService`/`VirtualRcInputService`/`KeyboardRcInputService`/`RcSource`/`ManualControlClient`/`ControlActionDispatcher` moved into its own `providers:` array from `rc-monitor.ts`, which is now a template-nested *child* with no `providers` of its own (hierarchical DI resolves its `inject()` calls to the same instances). **Lifecycle consequence stated plainly**: the Gamepad `requestAnimationFrame` poll loop now runs for the entire life of the cockpit route, not only while the Controller drawer was open — the HUD's own at-rest badge needs a live `rc.connected()` too. The three write-effects that used to live in `rc-monitor.ts` (`dispatcher.bind`/`dispatcher.setArmed`, `virtual.bindTo`/`clear`, `keyboard.bind`) moved with the providers and now have **exactly one** caller — `fly-hud.ts`'s own constructor — never duplicated; `rc-monitor.ts` keeps only read-only re-derivations of the same signals for its own rendering, safe to duplicate since a side-effect-free `computed()` can never race another.
    - **Zones** (§3's own frozen contract): bottom-center — three input-source pills (On-screen/Transmitter/Keys) locked for the life of a session (`fly-hud-logic.ts#sourceLocked`), and beside them either the at-rest **badge + Take-control pill**, or — once `client.state() === 'engaged'` — the **live input widget** (`fly-hud-logic.ts#hudElementsFrom`: horizontal bar for a `CENTERED` steering/yaw axis, vertical bar for a throttle with its own rest point at 0%/50% per travel, a 2D crosshair+knob pad for a roll/pitch pair — one render path, travel-shape-driven, not a fork per vehicle kind — or, in keyboard mode, a key-glyph ticker, `fly-hud-logic.ts#axisKeyGlyphs`, W/A/S/D always plus the arrow pad only on a two-pad aircraft layout) + a Release pill. Bottom-right — `<vision-flight-command-panel>`, unchanged component **narrowed to Arm/Disarm only**: mode picking split out into new `mode-picker.ts` (`<vision-mode-picker>`, still inside `<vision-rc-monitor>` — a deliberate, infrequent dropdown choice, not the "one live action" R2's QGC/DJI/Betaflight survey puts on video), reached through a new `.hud-arm-zone` wrapper `flight-command-panel.html` grows around its own `.command-row` (that component is `:host{display:contents}`, so `fly-hud.css`'s `.hud-arm` positions this wrapper, not `::ng-deep`). A new documented z-index literal, `.hud-bottom-center`/`.hud-arm = 8` (`fly-hud.css`), sits above `.main-secondary` (4) and every other passive `.main-*` card, below the tool-rail drawers (120) and `.hud-confirm` (20). Status: armed/mode chips join the existing `.main-header` cluster (`cockpit.ts#headerArmedChip`/`headerMode`/`headerModeStale`, reusing `rc-monitor-logic.ts#armedChip`/`sampleIsStale` verbatim — a second call site against the same pure rule, never a re-derived copy) rather than opening a new zone. Transients (`Control released — failsafe took over`/`Control denied — …`) are now `ToastService` calls (`fly-hud-logic.ts#hudTransitionToast`, an edge-detected effect over `client.state()`/`deniedReason()`/`watchdogTripped()`), never persistent video text.
    - **`rc-monitor` demoted to purely informational** (its own class doc comment's new "§0's reversal" section) — transmitter picture, `<vision-mode-picker>`, the asset session link (Z1's "Engage link"/"End session"), device/rate/latency chips, hints, diagnostics. **Lost**: the sticky Take-control/Release footer and the combined mode/arm/disarm panel — both now live on the HUD. `disabledReason`/`block`/`_readiness` still compute here off the exact same pure functions the HUD calls for its own pill, but nothing here disables a button any more (there is none left to disable) — this component only ever renders their text, the full-sentence counterpart to whatever the HUD abbreviated to an icon/word/`title`. **Opening this drawer is never required to take control or arm.**
    - **The neutral-stick arm gate (§2, frozen).** New `core/rc/neutral-gate-logic.ts` (pure, Angular-free): `worstNeutralOffender(channelMap, axes, tolerancePercent)` ranks bound axes by *proportional* deviation from rest (a unidirectional throttle's rest-to-full span is twice a centred control's rest-to-either-end span, so raw `displayPercent` distance would unfairly favor flagging the throttle), `neutralOffenderReason` states the axis's own label + live value + `"center sticks to arm"`/`"throttle to zero to arm"` per its travel, `neutralGateReason(engageState, channelMap, axes, tolerancePercent)` is `undefined` outside an engaging/engaged session or once every bound axis reads neutral (gates only Arm, never Disarm/mode/RTL/emergency-stop — §2's frozen rule). `ThresholdsStore` gained the matching `rc: Signal<RcThresholds>` field (same fetch-once store, same `GET /api/ops/thresholds` call `battery` already reads — no second network round trip), degrading to `DEFAULT_RC_THRESHOLDS = {neutralTolerancePercent: 5}` on a failed fetch **or** a response that simply omits `rc` (BK1, the server-side wave that adds it, ships in parallel — an absent field is an expected shape here, not a fault). `fly-hud.ts#sticksNotNeutralReason` is the first real consumer of both pieces, forwarded to `<vision-flight-command-panel>` where `flight-command-panel-logic.ts#armDisableReason(groundedReason, sticksNotNeutralReason)` composes it with the pre-existing S1 grounding reason — **grounding wins** when both hold. `arm-confirm-dialog.{ts,html,css}` (pre-existing two-stage confirm, kept verbatim per §5's "out of scope") gained a live neutral-state line so the final confirm step shows the same fact the gate itself is keyed on, not just an inert warning.
    - **Contract friction, flagged per this wave's own instruction, not silently resolved** (`fly-hud.ts`'s own class doc comment carries both in full): (1) §3's literal at-rest text is "ONE frosted pill + one badge … nothing else new on video" — the three input-source pills render at rest too, **before** engaging, because `sourceLocked()` freezes the choice the instant a handshake starts; deferring the picker to post-engage would mean it could never be used at all. (2) §3 describes the HUD's own engaged widget as a read-only live readout, never a drag target — but the on-screen source's actual *interactive* stick surface (`vision-transmitter-view`'s `[interactive]` mode) still only exists inside `<vision-rc-monitor>`, so `selectSource('virtual')` also emits a new `openRcPanel` output (`cockpit.html` wires it to `panels.open('rc')`) — picking "On-screen" opens the one place it can actually be driven from, rather than silently selecting a source with nothing to move it.
    - **Fixed alongside**: `neutral-gate-logic.spec.ts` shipped with three failing cases from a prior session — `worstNeutralOffender`/`neutralGateReason` test fixtures built a single-binding `channelMap` whose one binding read `sourceIndex: 1` (mirroring a realistic two-axis rover map) but then passed a one-element `axes` array meant for index 0, so the binding's own value always read back as the array-out-of-bounds fallback (`0`, i.e. neutral) regardless of what the test intended to assert. Fixed by padding those `axes` arrays with an unclaimed index-0 filler, not by changing the shared `COPTER_THROTTLE`/`ROVER_THROTTLE` fixtures (both are also used at `sourceIndex: 1` alongside a real index-0 `STEERING` binding elsewhere in the same file, where the original indices are correct). `cockpit.css`'s pre-existing `.main-diagnostics` card (bottom-right) also moved up by `--space-64` (mirroring `.main-preflight`'s identical left-side convention) to stop it sharing a corner with the new `.hud-arm` zone.
    - **Left out, named honestly**: no live-fly or SITL verification of a bound switch through the new HUD widget — that gate is the operator's own step (repo-standing rule), not something this wave could exercise; dark theme and the `--bp-lg` collapse were checked against the token contract and existing `.surface-dark`/`--hud-*` precedent, not against a live rendered screenshot in either theme — no browser drive was performed this wave.
    - **Verify chain, all green**: `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`/`-p tsconfig.spec.json` — 0 errors both. `npm run test:ci` — **178/178 files, 3474/3474 tests** (includes the `neutral-gate-logic.spec.ts` fix above and new `fly-hud-logic.spec.ts#axisKeyGlyphs` cases). `npx ng build --configuration production` — green (via `npx`, not a bare `ng`; see the new Gotchas entry below), same two pre-existing budget warnings only. **Bundle delta**, measured against a disposable `git worktree add --detach <tmp> HEAD` baseline (symlinked `node_modules`, isolated from this tree's own pre-existing unrelated uncommitted changes in `infra/rover-sim/**`/`drone-link/**`): initial bundle **421.58 kB → 421.64 kB raw (+0.06 kB, effectively flat — everything here is lazy), 118.55 kB → 118.62 kB transfer (+0.07 kB)**. Lazy **`cockpit` chunk 162.84 kB → 178.87 kB raw (+16.03 kB), 35.41 kB → 37.99 kB transfer (+2.58 kB)** — the new `fly-hud` component + `neutral-gate-logic`/`fly-hud-logic` + the `mode-picker` split + the `rc-monitor`/`flight-command-panel`/`arm-confirm-dialog` edits all bundle into this one chunk.
  - **Wave FLY-FLOW W1-W3 (docs/plans/active/FLY-FLOW-PLAN.md — one bottom-center dock + left/right column dedupe, superseding two FLY-CONTROL-UX-PLAN.md §3 decisions by direct owner request).** A new pure `FlyStage = 'idle' | 'starting' | 'live' | 'engaged'` (`fly-logic.ts#flyStage`, priority `live → busy → operatorEngaged → idle`) and `CockpitFacade#stage` (computed over `live`/`stopped`/`busy`/`streamState`/`operatorEngaged`) now drive every zone that used to guess at "which of three near-identical states is this" independently.
    - **The dock (`cockpit.html`'s `.dock`/`cockpit.css`'s same class)** replaces three previously independent, colliding bottom-center overlays — `.stage-notice`, `.not-streaming-card`, and the `.grid-controls` grid row (Start/Stop/Replay) — with one flex column, `--space-8` gap: the mutually-exclusive notice line (stalled/reconnecting `.notice`, `.stream-state-chip`, `.detection-off-chip`, "no view URL yet" — unchanged conditions, now one shared slot) above, then the idle/starting **dock card** (`.dock-card`, ex-`.not-streaming-card`) below — last-seen/last-position + "Open on map ›", Start (poka-yoke disabled reason inline for a camera-less asset), and a quiet `.hint`-styled Replay-last-flight link (was `.btn.secondary`) with its kebab for older flights folded in. Renders only at `stage() === 'idle' || 'starting'`; empty at `live`/`engaged`, ceding the anchor to `<vision-fly-hud>`'s own zone below.
    - **`<vision-fly-hud>`'s `.hud-bottom-center`** is now gated on the identical `stage()` value (`'live' || 'engaged'`, forwarded via a new `stage` input — this component has no `CockpitFacade` dependency of its own, so it's a plain input, not an inject) rather than mounting unconditionally: silent at rest, one row once live. The three always-on `On-screen`/`Transmitter`/`Keys` `.source-pill` buttons collapse into one `.hud-pill.source-select` — a native `<select>` styled like `cockpit.css`'s `.switcher-control`/`.switcher` pair — locked (`sourceChoiceLocked()`) from the instant a handshake starts through `engaged`, same rule as before, now visibly disabled rather than three individually-greyed buttons. `(openRcPanel)` on picking On-screen is unchanged. Two component-scoped notions of "engaged" remain distinct on purpose (`stage()` from the facade vs. `client.state()` from this component's own `ManualControlClient`, a different injector) — see `fly-hud.ts`'s own `stage` doc comment.
    - **Header `armed`/`mode` chips deleted** (`cockpit.ts`'s `headerStatusVisible`/`headerArmedChip`/`headerMode(Stale)`, `cockpit.html`'s `.header-status`, `cockpit.css`'s same class) — both facts already render in `<vision-fly-osd>`'s Power/Nav groups one row below; this was a duplicate, not a second source.
    - **`.main-left`/`.main-right`** (`cockpit.css`) replace the three independent `position: absolute` overlays `.main-secondary`/`.main-map`/`.main-preflight` used to be, each guessing at its neighbor's height via an approximate `calc(--overlay-pad + --space-64)` offset — now one `column-reverse` flex column (bottom-up: secondary tiles → map inset → pre-flight checklist → the ticker overlay, DOM order top-to-bottom) with a real `--space-8` gap, one shared anchor/z-index. `.main-preflight`/`.main-diagnostics` are now bare `display: contents` structural wrappers (their own position/offset moved to the parent column; flex blockification keeps their single child a correct flex item with no HTML change needed). `.main-right` mirrors `.main-left` for diagnostics alone, still the fixed `--space-64` offset above `.hud-arm` (can't become a real `gap` — that's a different component's box).
    - **Map inset additionally gated on `CockpitFacade#hasKnownPosition`** (`hasFix(dronePosition())`, not just "is defined") — never renders world-zoomed on Null Island for an asset with no real fix.
    - **Pre-flight auto-collapse now stage-driven**: `preflightCollapsed = linkedSignal(() => stage() === 'live' || stage() === 'engaged')` (was `linkedSignal(() => live())`) — same collapse behavior, expressed against the one stage signal everything else now reads instead of a second ad-hoc read of `live()`.
    - **The events ticker restored**, not merely relocated — git archaeology during this wave found it had been deleted by an unrelated ad-hoc "UI desing" commit with no plan behind the removal, while `CockpitFacade#tickerEvents`/`tickerLabel` and `TICKER_MAX_EVENTS` (`fly-logic.ts`, now `3` not `4`) stayed live and fully wired the whole time. Now a transient, non-interactive (`pointer-events: none`, `aria-hidden`) overlay — the topmost member of `.main-left`'s stack, capped at 3 rows, `.ticker`/`.ticker-row` (new CSS, frosted-pill rows, open events read `--text` instead of `--text-muted`).
    - **Stop stream** moved off the deleted `.grid-controls` row onto a small `btn danger small` ghost (`.telemetry-stop`, absolutely positioned against the now-`position: relative` `.grid-telemetry`) at the OSD shelf's right edge — visible only `live && !watchMode`. Opens the identical `.hud-confirm` scrim, unchanged.
    - **Grid rows: five → three.** `.cockpit`'s `grid-template-areas` drops the `ticker`/`controls` rows outright (`"banner banner" / "main rail" / "telemetry rail"`) — both were folded into overlays above, not deleted content.
    - **Deviations from FLY-CONTROL-UX-PLAN.md §3, by direct owner request (FLY-FLOW-PLAN.md is authoritative over both)**: (1) the three source pills are no longer *always* visible — they render only once `live`/`engaged`, not at rest; (2) the header no longer carries its own armed/mode chip pair. Both are named here per that plan's own still-valid "flag, don't silently resolve" instruction, now superseded.
    - **Ambiguity resolved toward the least at-rest UI** (this wave's own tie-break rule) in two more places: the source-select stays visible-but-disabled through `engaged` (matches the "same lock rule" text, judged more honest than hiding the control the disabled state is describing) rather than disappearing; `stage()` flips straight to `'live'` the instant `live()` is true regardless of `streamState` (never a stale "Not streaming" card once genuinely live — CLAUDE.md rule 9), leaving "waiting for first frame" to the existing `videoNotice()` notice line instead of a fourth stage value.
    - **Dev parity / role-gating**: untouched — no signal this wave reads or gates on is auth- or role-derived; `vision.auth.enabled=false` behaves identically before and after.
    - **Verify chain, all green**: `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`/`-p tsconfig.spec.json` — 0 errors both. `npm run test:ci` — **178/178 files, 3482/3482 tests**, no spec changes needed (no test referenced the deleted `.grid-controls`/`.stage-notice`/`.not-streaming-card`/`.header-status` markup directly). `ng build --configuration production` — green, budgets unchanged from pre-existing warnings (initial bundle already over budget before this wave); **bundle delta measured against `master`: initial total 421.64 kB → 421.73 kB raw (+0.09 kB), 118.62 kB → 118.65 kB transfer (+0.03 kB); `cockpit` lazy chunk 178.87 kB → 178.61 kB raw (−0.26 kB), 37.99 kB → 38.01 kB transfer (+0.02 kB)** — net flat: the deleted zones' markup/CSS roughly offset the new dock/ticker/source-select.
  - **Wave FLY-FLOW W4 (docs/plans/active/FLY-FLOW-PLAN.md §4 W4 — owner live-review feedback, appended to the plan and committed as authoritative).** Three changes, all owner-specified: an honest map slot, the "connect ritual" replacing direct-engage, and pre-flight moving off the video glass.
    - **Honest map slot (item 1)** — `cockpit.html`'s `@if (facade.mapVisible() && facade.hasTelemetryDevice())` no longer also requires `hasKnownPosition()` to render `.main-map` at all; that check moved *inside* the block as a nested `@if`/`@else`: `<vision-tactical-map>` once a real fix exists, else a new `.main-map-placeholder` (`cockpit.css`, `--hud-*` frosted, 220px tall — the same footprint `tactical-map.css`'s own follow-mode settles into, so a fix arriving never visibly jumps the layout) reading "No position yet — the map follows this drone once it reports a fix." The header's map-toggle button now always visibly connects to something once turned on. The D5 world-zoomed-empty-Leaflet ban stays exactly in force — `<vision-tactical-map>` itself is still never mounted without a real fix.
    - **The connect ritual (item 2)** — new 3-file `take-control-modal.{ts,html,css}` in `features/fly/`, mounted inside `<vision-fly-hud>`'s own template (not `cockpit.ts`) so it inherits that component's RC provider stack (`RcInputService`/`RcSource`/`ManualControlClient`) via hierarchical DI with no `@Input()` plumbing. The at-rest dock now reduces to `[badge] [Take control]` only — `fly-hud.html`'s unconditional `.hud-pill.source-select` moved to render only inside the `client.state() === 'engaged'` branch, its at-rest twin deleted outright. Clicking Take control opens the modal (`fly-hud.ts#openTakeControl`) instead of engaging directly; same overlay family as `cv-setup-modal` (`.backdrop`/`.dialog`, z-index 150, `--scrim-strong`/`--panel`). Modal contents top-to-bottom: `<vision-preflight-checklist>` (reused verbatim, not collapsible), a "Control with" three-tile picker (frontend-style §4's 2px-inset-bar + `--color-info-soft` selection language, the identical recipe `wizard-step.css`'s own tiles use) defaulting to `RcSource.kind()` at construction (free "remember the last choice" — that service never resets its own `_kind` on release), and a primary "Start control" button. Confirming (`(confirmed)="confirmTakeControl($event)"`) reuses `fly-hud.ts`'s own already-tested `selectSource`/`requestEngage` verbatim rather than duplicating the commit+engage sequence — picking On-screen still opens the informational rail exactly as before. Honest denial renders inline (`client.deniedReason()`), never a silent spinner; a new constructor `effect()` in `fly-hud.ts` auto-closes the modal once `client.state() === 'engaged'`, leaving it open on `'denied'` so the reason stays visible. Cancel/backdrop-click/header-×/`Esc` all only hide the modal, never call `client.release()` — closing is not a promise to abort an in-flight handshake.
      - **Two independent gates, not one shared gate** — reusing the pre-W4 `engageDisabledReason`/`EngageGateInput` unchanged for the outer pill would have recreated a dead end: an operator whose committed source was a disconnected gamepad would find the pill disabled with no way left to reach the picker that could fix it, since that picker is now inside the very modal the pill opens. New `openTakeControlDisabledReason`/`OpenTakeControlGateInput` (`core/rc/fly-hud-logic.ts`, `+4` spec cases) is deliberately narrower — only `canCommand` and an already in-flight handshake block the pill — mirroring `hudBadgeFor`'s own "hasAsset never applies here" precedent. The modal's own "Start control" button keeps the full, unmodified `engageDisabledReason` gate, evaluated against its own **local, uncommitted** `selected()` signal rather than `RcSource.kind()`, so switching tiles updates the button's own disabled state before Start is ever clicked.
      - **Escape handling deliberately deviates** from the app's "one page-level `document` keydown listener" convention (`cockpit.ts` owns that one, `CvSetupModal` explicitly opts out of a local listener of its own) — `TakeControlModal` lives inside `FlyHud`'s own component-scoped injector, unreachable from `cockpit.ts`'s `collapseOverlays()` cascade without a new `viewChild(FlyHud)` + public method just for one key. It adds its own scoped listener instead, safe because the component only ever mounts via `@if` while open (`DestroyRef` removes the listener on unmount). Flagged, accepted trade-off: a tool-rail drawer left open behind this modal would see both listeners react to one `Esc` press (`cockpit.ts` closing the drawer, this one closing itself) — narrow, cosmetic, never a functional bug.
    - **Pre-flight off the glass (item 3)** — `cockpit.html`'s `.main-preflight` floating chip block deleted outright (not merely hidden); `<vision-preflight-checklist>` now renders in exactly two places: inside the new modal (above), and as one quiet line on the idle/starting dock card — `facade.preflightDockSummary()` (new `CockpitFacade` computed, wrapping new `fly-logic.ts#dockPreflightSummaryLabel` over the pre-existing `flight-state-logic.ts#preflightSummary` rollup, itself newly promoted to a `CockpitFacade#preflightSummary` computed so the modal and the dock line share one call rather than two that could drift). Reads `'Pre-flight complete'` once every row clears, else `'Pre-flight: ' + label` — deliberately never softens a real `'fail'` blocker into the "unchecked" wording (CLAUDE.md rule 9): a fail summary reads `'Pre-flight: N blockers'`, keeping `preflightSummary`'s own worst-state-wins label. `preflightItems`/`derivePreflight` themselves are untouched — only the floating-chip presentation moved. The chip's own show/collapse machinery (`CockpitFacade#showPreflightChecklist`/`preflightCollapsed`, a `linkedSignal` over `stage()`) had no remaining caller once the chip's HTML was deleted and was removed outright rather than left dead, along with `cockpit.ts`'s now-unused `PreflightChecklist` import and `cockpit-facade.ts`'s now-unused `linkedSignal` import.
    - **Dev parity / role-gating**: untouched — every signal this wave reads or gates on (`canCommand`, `client.state()`, `hasKnownPosition()`, telemetry) is identical to W1-W3's own set; `vision.auth.enabled=false` behaves identically before and after.
    - **Ambiguity resolved toward the least at-rest UI** (this wave's own tie-break rule, continuing W1-W3's): the modal's checklist stays always-expanded (`collapsible` left at its default `false`) rather than adding a new collapse affordance the spec's "interactive" wording didn't clearly ask for; "Control with" tile selection is purely local/uncommitted until Start control fires, so browsing tiles can never be observed by anything downstream.
    - **Verify chain, all green**: `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`/`-p tsconfig.spec.json` — 0 errors both. `npm run test:ci` — **178/178 files, 3489/3489 tests** (new `fly-hud-logic.spec.ts#openTakeControlDisabledReason` + `fly-logic.spec.ts#dockPreflightSummaryLabel` cases; no existing spec touched the deleted `.main-preflight`/at-rest-source-select markup directly). `ng build --configuration production` — green, same two pre-existing budget warnings only; **bundle delta measured against this branch's own pre-W4 tip (`git stash`/rebuild/pop, not `master`, since W1-W3 are ahead of `master` on this branch): initial total 421.73 kB → 421.82 kB raw (+0.09 kB), 118.63 kB → 118.69 kB transfer (+0.06 kB); `cockpit` lazy chunk 178.77 kB → 185.76 kB raw (+6.99 kB), 38.03 kB → 38.92 kB transfer (+0.89 kB)** — the new modal component plus its CSS account for essentially all of it, offset slightly by the deleted floating-chip markup/signals.
  - **Wave W5 (docs/plans/active/SOURCE-ONBOARDING-2-PLAN.md §3.3 "the terminal action" + D6/D7), scoped entirely to `features/fly/**`** — one shared tree with W1 (`shared/ui/step-rail`, `core/onboarding/**`, `core/discovery/**`), W2/W3 (`features/onboarding/**`) and W4 (`features/inventory|devices|asset-detail/**`, its own new sub-bullets/Status entry above) running concurrently; disjointness confirmed both by `git status` throughout and by a pathspec-scoped `git stash push -- src/app/features/fly` isolation check (see Verify chain below).
    - **`?autostart=1` consumed exactly once.** New `fly-logic.ts#isAutostart(param)` sits directly under `isWatchMode` and mirrors it verbatim (`param === '1'`, nothing else counts — a query param the onboarding wizard's Ready screen builds for itself, `Open cockpit › → /fly/:assetId?autostart=1`, never one a person types). `CockpitPage` gains a route-bound `autostart` input, the same `input()` pattern as its existing `watch` input (wired automatically by `withComponentInputBinding()`), forwarded via a new `CockpitFacade#setAutostart` to a new `autostartSignal`. The one-shot effect near the end of the facade's constructor guards with a **plain field**, `autostartHandled: boolean` — not a signal, since flipping it must never itself retrigger the effect — set `true` **before** either async call so `engageSession()`'s own success path re-fetching `asset()` can never re-enter (a bare unguarded `effect()` re-fires on *any* signal it reads, including its own side effects' consequences). It waits for `asset()`/`loadError()` to settle before deciding anything — deciding while the asset load is still in flight would misread "not loaded yet" as "not fitted" and silently skip a real half, a fabricated-by-omission attempt (CLAUDE.md rule 9) — then calls exactly `start()` if `primaryDevice()` is set and `engageSession()` if `hasTelemetryDevice()` is true: the *same* calls a manual click on those buttons makes, so a grounding denial (docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md S1) or any other failure surfaces through their own existing toast/silent-degrade paths — no second, competing denial UI was built. A half with no matching device is never attempted at all. Finishes with `router.navigate([], { relativeTo: route, queryParams: { autostart: null }, queryParamsHandling: 'merge', replaceUrl: true })`, the same one-shot query-param-strip idiom as `inventory-facade.ts`/`devices-facade.ts`/`command-facade.ts`/`roster-facade.ts`/`alerts-facade.ts`, so reloading the resulting URL never re-fires.
    - **The cockpit's own synthetic-half badge (D6).** `cockpit.html` reads `device.origin === 'SIMULATED'` **per device**, never per asset/category — a vehicle can be half real (a real autopilot with a synthetic camera), the same D6 posture wave W4's own badges above take on the Inventory/Devices/Asset-detail pages, applied here to the one surface those don't cover. Two new, honest, click-nothing indicators, both frosted `--hud-*` chrome (legal here — the cockpit is a `.surface-dark` root, confirmed in `cockpit.html`): a header `.origin-chip` pill ("Simulated feed") beside the map-toggle button, shown only when `facade.primaryDevice()?.origin === 'SIMULATED'`; and a quieter `.tile-sim` suffix ("· Simulated", `color-mix(in srgb, var(--white) 70%, transparent)`) appended to each secondary device tile's own label when that specific device's `origin` is `'SIMULATED'`.
    - **D7 — the dangling pilot link.** `drone-picker.html`'s **grouped** "Your vehicles" empty leg carried a second, ungated `Add source ›` link, distinct from the top-level empty-picker state's already-gated one (`fly-logic.ts#pickerEmptyStateCopy`'s `showAddSource`): a PILOT with no assignments yet but at least one simulated asset in view saw a link guaranteed to bounce them off `/add-source`'s `orgGuard`. New `DronePickerFacade#canManageOrg` (`hasCapability(auth.capabilities(), 'MANAGE_ORG')` — the identical gate `pickerEmptyStateCopy` already uses, one capability check read the same way everywhere) branches the empty-line copy: a manager still sees "No real vehicles yet — Add source ›"; anyone else sees "No real vehicles yet — ask a fleet manager to add one." `orgGuard` on `/add-source` is untouched, per the plan's explicit instruction (§3.5 D7) — the guard was correct, the dangling door was the defect.
    - **Dev parity / role-gating**: `vision.auth.enabled=false`'s dev admin resolves `MANAGE_ORG` unbounded exactly as before, so `canManageOrg()` is `true` and the gated link renders identically to pre-wave behavior; the autostart effect and both origin badges read no role or capability at all.
    - **Tests**: `fly-logic.spec.ts` gains `isAutostart`'s exact-match cases (mirroring `isWatchMode`'s own describe block). `drone-picker.spec.ts` gains one case proving a PILOT sees the ask-a-manager copy with no `<a>` inside `.picker-group-empty`.
    - **Verify chain**: `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`/`-p tsconfig.spec.json` — 0 errors both, and `npm run test:ci` — **183/183 files, 3658/3658 tests** green, both at the moment this wave's own edits were completed. Re-running either check afterward, on this same shared tree, surfaces compile errors **entirely inside `features/onboarding/**`** (`WizardStep`/`onboarding-facade.ts`/`onboarding-store.ts` in a transiently-inconsistent state — the identical concurrent-agent blocker wave W4's own Status entry above already documents in detail) — confirmed unrelated to this wave by the same isolation technique that entry used: a pathspec-scoped `git stash push --include-untracked -- src/app/features/fly` reproduces the identical onboarding-only error set with this wave's nine files completely absent from the tree, and a plain grep confirms no file under `features/fly/**` imports anything from `features/onboarding/**`. **`ng build --configuration production` could not be completed or measured this wave**, for the same reason — recommend re-running it, and re-measuring the bundle delta via the same `git stash`-scoped method every prior `fly/` wave above used, once the onboarding waves in flight land.
- **live/** (`/live/:deviceId`) — `LivePage`/`LiveFacade`: the single-device, video-first watch page with a collapsible rail (docs/main/CYCLES-PLAN.md §2, §9). `TelemetryStore`/`DetectionsStore` live in `LivePage`'s own `providers` (one poller-set per route activation) so the facade and this page's child components (`TelemetryOsd`, `DetectionsStrip`, `TacticalMap` map inset + `MapTools` drawer, `StreamInfoPanel`) keep DI-sharing the same store instances. No `*-logic.ts` of its own yet — its pure formatting (declutter labels) is shared from `shared/player/detection-overlay-logic.ts`.
  - **Follow reaches Live (docs/plans/active/TRACK-FOLLOW-PLAN.md §3, wave W5, D6).** `LiveFacade` computes `lockedTrackId`/`follow`/`lostBox` off its own `DetectionsStore` the same way `CockpitFacade` does — `lockedTrackId` off the per-frame feed's `tracking.lockedTrackId` (D1's fix, mirrored here from day one), `follow`/`lostBox` off the richer `GET .../tracks` poll driven by a constructor effect calling `followTracks(streamId, wantsTracksPoll())`. `wantsTracksPoll` here is simply `live()` — Live has no Vision drawer to gate on the way Fly does. `<vision-player>` gains `[lockedTrackId]`/`[lostBox]`/`(trackFollowed)`; `<vision-follow-hud>` mounts right after the player, `[canRelease]` hardcoded `true` — Live has no watch-mode/role concept today (grep-confirmed: no `watchMode`/`topRole` anywhere in this feature), and every viewer here already has full Start/Stop control, so gating Release would be dishonest rather than safer. `followTrack`/`releaseFollow`/`reacquireFollow` reuse `features/fly/cv-control-panel-logic.ts#buildFollowLockPatch`/`buildReleaseLockPatch` — a deliberate cross-feature import (not restricted by `architecture.spec.ts`, which only scans routed pages' own store/API injection, not a facade's pure-function imports) so Fly's two doors and Live's one door can never disagree on PATCH shape.
