- **crew/** (`/crew/:assetId`, `docs/plans/active/CREW-CONTROL-PLAN.md` §3.4, wave W3) — `CrewSeatPage`/`CrewFacade`: a second, camera-only station on an asset a pilot may already be flying from `/fly/:assetId` at the same time — full-bleed video + boxes, the CV control body, detections strip, and map-tools, with **zero flight verbs anywhere in the template** (no `<vision-fly-hud>`, no arm/disarm/mode/RTH/e-stop/aux, no RC engage, no session engage/disengage — every one of those stays `/fly`-only). Structurally `LiveFacade`'s own shape (follow-lock trio, telemetry/detections track/reset effects), visually `cockpit.html`'s own full-bleed L0-L3 grid (header/dock/rail overlaying a `.grid-main` video hero) simplified by one layer — no failsafe/grounded banner row, no bottom OSD shelf (the read-only telemetry readout is a `.crew-telemetry` corner overlay instead, gated on `hasTelemetryDevice()`, a considered-but-not-explicitly-specified placement since the plan's own L0-L3 vocabulary names no shelf for this page). `CrewPanelId = 'vision' | 'map'` — exactly two tool-rail doors (§3.4's frozen composition table has no flight/rc/help door here); the Vision drawer mounts the unmodified `<vision-cv-control-panel>` inside a `.panel-inert` wrapper (`pointer-events:none`, dimmed, a reason line above it) whenever `stage() === 'C2'` — that shared component has no `disabled`/`readOnly` input of its own and is out of this wave's file scope, so the inert posture is achieved entirely from this page's own CSS instead. `<vision-detections-strip>`'s interactive class-hide write (`[streamId]`) is bound only at `C3`, reverting to the strip's own pre-existing read-only chip mode at every other stage — the identical dual-mode contract `cockpit.html` already relies on. `<vision-map-tools>` mounts with `{marks:true, layers:'manage', draw:true, zones:true, cockpit:{assetId, dronePosition}}` and no `[map]` binding (no `<vision-tactical-map>` on this page, mirroring `cockpit.html`'s own `marks` door) — `GeoStore` is **deliberately not injected** (nothing in this page's composition table reads visual-geo corrections, unlike `CockpitFacade`'s own separate map inset). Pure: `crew-logic.ts` — `CrewStage = 'C0'|'C1'|'C2'|'C3'` (`crewStage(input)`, `live`→`busy`→`C0` priority, one stage fewer than `fly-logic.ts#FlyStage` — no `'engaged'` counterpart, this page never commands flight), `cameraHeldByOther(camera)` (**`camera.holderUserId !== null && !camera.mine`, never bare `!camera.mine`** — a genuinely free seat also reads `mine: false`, §3.6's wire contract), `crewDock(stage, camera)` (§3.4's four-row table, copy verbatim — C0 offers "Start video" or names the holder in a reason, never both; C2/C3 are fixed phrases, never interpolated with a name), `cameraSeatChipLabel(camera)` (You/Free/holder name, the header's own persistent seat readout). `canRelease` on this page's `<vision-follow-hud>` is a real `stage() === 'C3'` gate — resolving `LiveFacade.canRelease`'s own doc comment, which explicitly foreshadowed this plan tightening it, but only for this page (`live.ts`/`live-facade.ts` themselves untouched, out of scope). No crew-specific landing/picker page exists yet (§0.3's own "landing → the assets assigned to them" journey step is not built) — the bare `/crew` path (`crew.routes.ts`) redirects to `/wall` today, purely so `nav-entries.ts`'s new ungated **"Crew seat"** entry (Operate group — named "Crew seat", not the bare "Crew", to avoid colliding with the pre-existing `roster/crew.ts`'s own `CrewPage` class/nav label at `/manage/roster`; this page's own routed component is `CrewSeatPage` for the same reason) has somewhere real to land.
- **core/seat/** (`docs/plans/active/CREW-CONTROL-PLAN.md` §3.6, wave W3) — `SeatStore` (page-provided, not `providedIn:'root'`, `CrewPage`'s only consumer today): the same `track(id)`/`reset()`/generation-counter lifecycle `GeoStore` established, plus a **second, independent, dynamically-registered `PollScheduler` task** for renew-while-mine — a fixed 3s task purely observes (`GET /api/assets/{id}/seats`), while a separate task re-registers itself only when `renewalIntervalMs(servedTtlMs)` (`= max(1000, round(ttlMs/3/1000)*1000)` — never hardcoded, re-derived from whatever `ttlMs` the server last served) actually changes, and on each tick `POST`s a renew-or-take (`takeAssetSeat`) for every seat kind the last-known response says is `mine`. A 409 on a renewal is treated as authoritative (re-reads via `getAssetSeats` rather than reconciling locally) — the losing side finds out it no longer holds the seat within one renewal tick, not the next 3s poll. Degrades to `seat-logic.ts#singleOperatorSeats(assetId)` (both seats free, every `may*` `true`, `mayForceSeat` `false`) on a 404/failure with no prior data — §3.8's guardrail (`vision.crew.enabled=false` ⇒ every read reports both seats free) reaches the frontend automatically through the same honest-fallback path a genuine network failure takes, no separate feature-flag branch needed. `CrewFacade` funnels every CV write path (the panel's own edits, the strip's label-hide write, `setDetection`, `start`/`followTrack`/`releaseFollow`) through `refreshStreamConfig()`/explicit `seats.refreshNow()` calls so a seat conflict's `stage()` flip is visible within one round trip rather than waiting out the ordinary 3s poll. `core/api/models.ts` gained `SeatKind`/`SeatHolderResponse`/`SeatsResponse`/`TakeSeatRequest` (§3.6's wire contract, byte-exact — explicit-null holder fields, `may*` authority booleans that are purely server-derived, **never** re-derived from role/capability on this side); `VisionApi` gained `getAssetSeats`/`takeAssetSeat`/`releaseAssetSeat` (the last one defined for API completeness/a future explicit-release affordance — nothing calls it yet, since §3.2 rule 5 makes silence-releases the only release mechanism this wave uses).
- **command/** — `CommandPage`/`CommandFacade` over `TacticalMap`; `asset-panel`, `zones-panel`, `setup-checklist`, `geofence-zone-dialog`, `rail-row` (`<vision-command-rail-row>`, new wave W2). Pure: `command-logic`. **`marks-panel` moved to `shared/map/map-controls/marks-panel/` (wave W2)** — see the `shared/` → `map/` bullet below; Command's own "Map tools" HUD button (bottom-right map corner — `<vision-tactical-map>` isn't in that wave's file scope to grow a fifth chrome corner) replaces the four former topbar buttons/overlays. **`AssetPanel`'s Telemetry tab gained a Route control (docs/plans/active/COMMAND-MAP-FLOW-PLAN.md §3.4, wave W3)** — five new plain inputs (`routeSpan`/`routes`/`routesLoading`/`routesError`/`routesNoUsages`) + one output (`routeSpanChanged`), fed entirely from `CommandFacade`'s `RouteStore` orchestration; the panel stays "deliberately dumb" (no store injected, same discipline as every other fact it shows). `CommandFacade#markers` now runs through `withLastContact`/`resolveLastContact` (closing wave W1's own fallback gap for the offline bucket — see the Status entry below).
  - **One attention verdict, rail triages like `/fly` (docs/plans/active/OPERATOR-UX-4-PLAN.md findings N2/N3/N4, §2, wave W2).** `CommandFacade#attentionByAssetId` (`assetId → EntityRow`, built once off the same `entityRows` the rail already sorted) is now the *only* place an asset's attention reasons/severity are read — fixes N2, a live-reproduced bug where the rail chip read CRIT (a `geofence-breach`, threaded into `buildEntityRows` from `LiveStore`) while `AssetPanel`'s own narrower `attentionReasons(asset, marker()?.gpsFixType)` call (missing `geofenceBreaches`/`pipelineErrorDetail` entirely) showed "All quiet" for the identical asset. `AssetPanel` now takes `[reasons]` as a plain `input.required<readonly AttentionReason[]>()` fed from `CommandFacade#selectedAttentionReasons` instead of deriving its own — one verdict, one source, so this class of drift is impossible by construction rather than patched for this one case. `core/fleet/attention-logic.ts#gpsDegradedReason` also gained a live-only gate (`!asset.streaming → undefined`, matching `telemetryReason`'s existing rule) — an OFFLINE asset is never CRIT/WARN for lacking a GPS fix it was never trying to get.
  - The rail (`command.html`) is now two structural-label groups, **Your vehicles (n)** / **Simulated (n)** — `CommandFacade#railGroups` (`command-logic.ts#buildRailGroups`, calling `core/fleet/triage-logic.ts#groupAndSort` with `rowRank` as the new optional `attentionRank` argument) orders each group streaming → needs attention (severity desc) → last seen desc → never-seen last. A **Hide simulated** checkbox reads/writes the *same* `vision.fly.hideSimulated` persisted flag `/fly`'s own picker uses (`CommandFacade#hideSimulated`/`#toggleHideSimulated`) — one preference, both pages. Offline rows show honest `Offline · 3d` age text (`RailRow.offlineAge`, via `core/fleet/triage-logic.ts#offlineLabel`) in place of the bare status word; the severity chip stays the row's only chip (frontend-style §5). Row markup extracted to `<vision-command-rail-row>` (`rail-row.ts/html/css`) — a dumb `[row]`/`[selected]`/`(select)` component, mirroring `DronePickerCard`'s own precedent (a plain child component over `NgTemplateOutlet` context typing) so the identical row renders under both groups without duplicating markup.
  - `attentionAgeLabel` (`core/fleet/attention-logic.ts`) now renders through `humanAge` (`… 3d ago`, zero remainders dropped) instead of `stream-info-logic.ts#formatDuration` (session-duration register, wrong for a telemetry sample that can legitimately be days stale).
- **wall/** (`/wall`, docs/plans/active/WALL-FLOW-PLAN.md, waves W1–W5) — **the Wall**, rebuilt as a grid of every live tile at once instead of the old fixed-column layout + always-open events rail. `WallPage`/`WallFacade` is the one frozen surface (§3.4) every other file in this folder is dumb against: a single 5s `api.fleetSummary()` poll (the exact `command-facade.ts#SUMMARY_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`/silent-degrade-on-background-failure precedent) replaces the old per-tile `TelemetryStore` polling entirely — `core/fleet/attention-logic.ts#attentionReasons`, `core/system-events/system-events-logic.ts#activePipelineErrorMessagesByStreamId`, and `core/geofence/geofence-logic.ts#activeGeofenceBreaches` all fold into one `wall-logic.ts#buildWallTiles(input): readonly WallTileModel[]` call alongside `EventsStore`'s shared detection-event feed. `WallTileModel` carries identity (`streamId`/`deviceId`/`assetId?`/`title`/`unlinked` — `unlinked` only ever asserts `true` once `BuildWallTilesInput.summaryLoaded` is `true`; before `WallFacade`'s own first fleet-summary fetch settles, "no asset data yet" is not the same claim as "confirmed no asset," a live-verification fix — see Gotchas), stream facts (`viewUrl?`/`whepUrl?`/`health: TileHealth` — `'live'|'starting'|'stalled'|'reconnecting'|'no-publisher'|'pipeline-error'|'unknown'`/`healthLabel`), attention (`severity`/`reasons: readonly AttentionReason[]`/`batteryLabel: string | null` — whole-percent, formatted once here (`"58%"`, never `AssetAttention.batteryPercent`'s raw float — a live tile once rendered `"58.349999999999994%"`), severity classification still reads the unrounded value/`batterySeverity`/`telemetryAgeLabel`/`flightMode?`/`armed?`/`failsafe?`), and a detection **pulse** (`pulse: TilePulse | null` — `{label, count, atIso}`, a 60s window, dominant-label-by-count with recency as the tiebreak — see `wall-logic.ts#tilePulse`'s own doc comment for why "most recent event" alone was wrong: count matters more than recency once two labels are both active). Tiles sort by `title` then `streamId` — stable, **never** attention-sorted, a deliberate divergence from Command's rail ("the wall is a map of the room," §3's frozen decision). Three density stops (`DENSITY_STOPS` — Comfortable/Compact/Dense, `tileMinPx` feeding `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(var(--tile-min), 1fr))`, D11 replacing the old `Tiles per row` `<select>`) and one shared `boxesMode` declutter level both alias `SettingsStore` directly (`wallDensity`, `declutterLevel` — the latter is H12's already-shared cross-page signal with `CockpitFacade`/`LiveFacade`, so cycling it here also changes Fly/Live). **P2 focus** (`vision-wall-focus`, `WallFacade#focus`/`clearFocus`/`focusedTile`) is an in-place overlay, never a route — `(openCockpit)`/`(watchLive)` are its only two navigations, both explicit labelled exits (§3 law 2: "nothing else on the wall navigates"); its own effect self-clears if the focused tile vanishes (stream stopped, summary poll caught up) rather than freezing on stale data. **Activity drawer** (`vision-wall-activity`, W4) replaces the old always-open events rail with an on-demand `vision-side-panel` scoped to *this wall's own streams, last hour* (`wall-logic.ts#wallActivityRows`, `ACTIVITY_WINDOW_MS = 3_600_000`) — deliberately less than the old rail (which showed every event including from removed devices), with an explicit "Older activity › Alerts center" footer link honest about being a window, not a history (§5 residual #2). `WallTile`/`WallFocus` each own a private `providers: [DetectionsStore]` for live per-frame detection boxes (`DetectionsStore.track(streamId, assetId?)`, no-op on an unchanged key) — the one thing a fleet-summary poll cannot supply. Pure: `wall-logic.ts` (`buildWallTiles`, `wallActivityRows`, `tileMinPx`, `DENSITY_STOPS`, `PULSE_WINDOW_MS`/`ACTIVITY_WINDOW_MS`). **`architecture.spec.ts`**: `wall/wall` is a routed page (facade + `Router` only); `wall-tile`/`wall-focus`/`wall-activity` are non-routed presentational children under that guard's own carve-out and DI-share `DetectionsStore`/`PollScheduler` directly, same precedent as every other non-routed child in this app.
  - **Follow, read-only (docs/plans/active/TRACK-FOLLOW-PLAN.md §3.3, wave W5) — the Wall stays a watch surface, never a control surface.** `WallTile` computes `lockedTrackId`/`follow` off its own `DetectionsStore` (`lockedTrackId` off the free per-frame feed already running for the box overlay; `follow` off `GET .../tracks`, gated by its own `wantsTracksPoll` — the **tightest** form of the formula in this plan: `lockedTrackId() !== 0 || follow()?.state === 'LOST'`, no broader "drawer open"/"merely live" clause, since Wall can show dozens of tiles at once and the overwhelming majority never hold a lock). The tracks-poll effect has its own explicit off-screen teardown (`followTracks(streamId, visible() && wantsTracksPoll())`) — it is a fully independent lifecycle from the box-overlay's own `track()`/`reset()` effect (`DetectionsStore`'s own doc comment), so `reset()` alone does not stop it. `<vision-follow-hud>` mounts as a DOM **sibling** of `.tile` (never a descendant — `.tile` is itself a `<button>`, and the HUD can render its own `<button>`, which nested inside would be invalid, click-bubbling HTML), positioned bottom-left via `:host ::ng-deep .follow-hud` (mirroring the existing top-left `.severity-dot`/top-right `.pulse-chip` absolute-badge idiom). `[canRelease]` is hardcoded `false` and neither `(release)` nor `(reacquire)` is bound — this mount can never send a write, by construction.
- **inventory/** (`/assets`, docs/plans/active/WAREHOUSE-UX-PLAN.md §3.1 rule 3/§3.3, wave W4) — **the Inventory page**. `InventoryPage`/`InventoryFacade` supersede `features/assets/**` (deleted outright this wave — its own table/filters/two-pane became this page's Vehicles tab, `assets-logic.ts` → `vehicles-logic.ts`) and fold in `features/devices/**`/`features/categories/**` as mounted tab content plus `features/reports/**`'s one surviving section (that page is otherwise deleted). One page, `?tab=vehicles|equipment|links|categories` (default `vehicles` — OQ4: `/assets` itself never changed URL, only its contents grew tabs). New `core/fleet/inventory-logic.ts` (framework-free, a **second-consumer** module per this file's own "moves to `core/` once a second consumer needs it" precedent) owns the cross-tab facts every tab shares: `parseInventoryTab`/`visibleInventoryTabs`/`isInventoryTabVisible` (Links/Categories are `canManageOrg`-gated **in the page**, not the route — a pilot who bookmarks `?tab=links` is clamped back to `vehicles` by `InventoryFacade#setTabFromQueryParam`, never bounced by a guard), `effectiveInventoryStateChip` (one merged lifecycle+inventory-state chip — archived/deactivated win outright, else `inventoryState`, `'unknown'` the honest fallback for a row whose inventory state was never fetched), and `inventoryExportFilename` (cosmetic only — the server's own `Content-Disposition` header wins whenever a browser honors both). **Wave W10 sweep (docs/plans/active/WAREHOUSE-UX-CONTEXT.md W10 findings A1/E1):** the Vehicles tab's "+ Add vehicle" button, the onboarding wizard's title, and its route title all now say **"Add vehicle"** consistently (were "+ Add source"/"Add a source" — a live-walkthrough copy inconsistency, A1). `vehicles-table.html`'s Readiness column (`<th>`/`<td>`) is now also gated `@if (connected())`, same as Firmware/Hours/Last flown/Links — the Equipment tab's non-connected categories (batteries, gimbals) never get a `GET /api/fleet/readiness` row at all, so the column used to render the literal wire value `'UNKNOWN'` as the word "Unknown" for every row, reading as a real (bad) verdict rather than "never evaluated" (E1).
  - **Vehicles/Equipment** (`VehiclesTable`, `<vision-vehicles-table [rows] [connected]>`) share one row shape and filter pipeline (`vehicles-logic.ts`). `InventoryFacade` fetches `assets`/`categories`/`users`/`readiness`/`fleetSummary` **once**; switching tabs only re-filters (`filterVehicleRowsByConnected` against `Category#connected`), never re-fetches — "one fetch, two views" (the facade's own doc comment). Columns: Name · Category · Serial · Readiness · Custodian · State chip, plus (Vehicles only) Firmware · Hours · Last flown · Links(n). Filters (shared across both tabs): search-by-name, category, inventory-state, custodian, readiness, show-retired/show-archived toggles (`InventoryFacade#hasActiveFilters`/`clearFilters`). The row kebab's verbs (`vehicles-logic.ts#vehicleRowActions` — a plain boolean set, poka-yoke *availability*: an inapplicable verb is hidden, never shown-disabled) are Issue to…/Return/Ground…/Release/Retire…/Open/Fly, each a thin `VisionApi#setAssetCustody`/`#setAssetInventory` call whose returned `AssetDetails` is spliced straight back into the loaded list (`InventoryFacade#patchAsset`) rather than re-fetching the whole fleet. **The detail panel gained Identity (Serial/Make/Model/Registration) and Custody (Custodian/Location/Since/Category) fact groups plus a Maintenance drawer** (docs/plans/active/WAREHOUSE-UX-PLAN.md §3.3's own "detail pane gains Identity and Custody fact groups and a Maintenance drawer") — the drawer is a lazy per-selected-asset `GET /api/assets/{id}/maintenance` (open/close a record inline, `MaintenanceKind` picker), fetched/cleared by a constructor `effect()` keyed on `selectedId()`, never a fleet-wide read (the same O(1)-not-O(assets) constraint `core/maintenance/maintenance-logic.ts`, wave W7, documents for its own page).
  - **"Fleet at a glance" KPI strip**, above the Vehicles tab only (`inventory-page-logic.ts#inventoryKpis`) — ported byte-for-byte from the deleted `features/reports/**`'s own KPI section (Total assets / Streaming now / Active / Deactivated / Needs attention). **Not carried forward**: Reports' "Assets by category" bar chart and "Needs attention" punch list — the Vehicles/Equipment table now sitting below already answers both (the category filter + column narrows by category; the inventory-state/readiness columns are the new *per-asset* form of "needs attention," a differently-scoped queue from `AssetAttention`'s own reasons vocabulary, which this table doesn't consume and which would otherwise duplicate that column).
  - **Export CSV** — a plain `<a [href]="facade.exportUrl()" [download]>`, `VisionApi#inventoryExportUrl('csv')` (pre-existing `InventoryExportController` endpoint), filename `inventory-YYYY-MM-DD.csv` (cosmetic only, see `inventoryExportFilename`'s own doc comment above).
  - **`<vision-found-devices>` — the discovery inbox's "Found devices" section (docs/plans/active/ZERO-CONFIG-ONBOARDING-CONTEXT.md §3 P2/§6/§11, wave Z2d).** Mounted with no inputs in `inventory.html` right below `<vision-page-bar>`, above the KPI strip/tab bar — fleet-wide, not tab-scoped, and renders nothing at all while `DiscoveryInboxStore.candidates()` is empty (`found-devices.html`'s outer `@if`). Backend contract (commit f09ec9b4): `GET /api/discovery/inbox` / `POST /api/discovery/inbox/{id}/register` / `POST /api/discovery/inbox/{id}/dismiss`, all `manageOrg`-gated server-side; **no SSE topic, poll-only v1** — the backend sweep runs every 30s so a 30s client poll (`DiscoveryInboxStore`, see the `core/**` stores table above) loses nothing. `found-devices.ts` injects `DiscoveryInboxStore`/`VisionApi`/`Router` directly (the `architecture.spec.ts` non-routed-child carve-out — it is a presentational child of the routed `InventoryPage`, not a route itself) and owns its own poll lifecycle independently of `InventoryFacade`: `store.activate()` in the constructor, released via `DestroyRef.onDestroy`.
    - **Card grid** (`found-device-card.ts`, dumb `OnPush`, one `<vision-found-device-card>` per visible candidate) shows name, a method chip (`discoveryMethodLabel`: MAVLink/ONVIF/mDNS/V4L2/Mediamtx push), address, suggested protocol when present, and `candidateAgeLabel` — "last heard 12s ago" via `humanAge`, never "online" (stale-not-live). Status-aware: `NEW` shows the action row; `REGISTERED` shows `Open asset ›` (`routerLink="/assets/:id"`) when `registeredAssetId` is present, else plain "Registered" text; `DISMISSED` shows plain "Dismissed" text. A header count badge (`.chip.accent`, NEW count) plus two independent "Show resolved"/"Show dismissed" toggles (`discovery-inbox-logic.ts#visibleCandidates`/`DiscoveryInboxVisibility`) gate the REGISTERED/DISMISSED groups out of the grid by default; NEW is always shown.
    - **Add** (`add-candidate-dialog.ts`) — small confirm dialog, not the full onboarding wizard: `displayName` (default = candidate name) + category `<select>` (default = `suggestedCategory` when present, via `defaultRegisterDraft`/`linkedSignal`) → `store.register(id, buildRegisterCommand(draft))` → `POST /api/discovery/inbox/{id}/register` → on success, `store.refresh()` re-fetches the real inbox (no guessed status) and `found-devices.ts` routes to `/assets/:assetId` off the real response.
    - **Attach to existing asset** (`attach-candidate-dialog.ts`) — asset `<select>` → `DiscoveryInboxStore#attach(id, deviceSpec, assetId)`. **No raw-spec attach endpoint exists** (`AssignDeviceRequest` only ever accepted `{deviceId}`) — `attach()` is a two-call flow: `registerDevice(buildDeviceSpecFromCandidate(candidate))` (creates an unowned `Device` from the candidate's `suggestedStream`, `capabilities` deliberately omitted so `RegisterDeviceRequest`'s server-side protocol default applies — `mavlink→[TELEMETRY]`, else `[VIDEO]`, `core/api/models.ts`'s own doc comment) then `assignDevice(assetId, device.id)` (the pre-existing endpoint, unchanged). **Never patches the candidate's status client-side** — `buildDeviceSpecFromCandidate`'s own TSDoc states the backend's next 30s sweep flips it to `REGISTERED` server-side (the dedupe rule); the UI just closes the dialog and lets the next poll show the real state. `canAttach` (`candidateActions`) additionally requires both `suggestedStreamProtocol` and `suggestedStreamUri` on the candidate.
    - **Dismiss** — one click, no confirm dialog, reversible in spirit via the "Show dismissed" toggle; `store.dismiss(id)` patches `candidatesSignal` with the real server-returned candidate from `POST .../dismiss`'s response body (not a guessed status either).
    - All three mutations go through `DiscoveryInboxStore`'s private `run<T>()` — the same toast-on-failure/no-optimistic-state idiom as `FleetStore`.
  - **Inbox honesty (A3, docs/plans/active/ASSET-FLOWS-PLAN.md §2)** — `GET /api/discovery/inbox` now answers `{candidates, sources}`; `found-devices.ts#unreachableWarnings` (`discovery-inbox-logic.ts#sourceUnreachableWarnings(store.sources())`) renders one `<vision-notice variant="warn" icon="alert">` per source reporting `UNREACHABLE` (e.g. "Mediamtx push unreachable — found devices may be incomplete."). Visibility is three-way, per the plan's own rule: **candidates=0 + all sources OK** → the section stays fully invisible, unchanged from before A3; **candidates=0 + some source unreachable** → the section becomes visible showing *only* the warning banner(s), no header/grid/empty-line; **candidates>0** → the existing header+grid (or "Nothing new…" line) renders exactly as before, with any warning banner(s) stacked above it. `found-devices.html`'s outer `@if` gate changed from `store.candidates().length > 0` to `… || unreachableWarnings().length > 0` to make the middle case possible; the pre-existing header/grid/empty-line markup moved into its own inner `@if (store.candidates().length > 0)` unchanged.
  - **Links mounts `DevicesPage` verbatim, Categories mounts `CategoriesPage` verbatim** — neither page was copied or rewritten; both keep their own facade (`DevicesFacade`/`CategoriesFacade`) and route file (`devices.routes.ts`/`categories.routes.ts`, now redirects — see Routes above), so `core/ui/architecture.spec.ts`'s facade/`UiStore` discipline still applies to them unchanged (its `ROUTED_PAGES` list keeps both, alongside the new `inventory/inventory`, and drops `assets/assets`). **The double page-bar is fixed (wave W9, docs/plans/active/WAREHOUSE-UX-CONTEXT.md "W8 → W9 handoff")** — `DevicesPage` and `CategoriesPage` both gained the same `embedded = input(false)` `RosterPage`/`OrgSettingsPage` set in wave W7 (see the `roster/, org-settings/` bullet below for the original precedent): `[class.page]="!embedded()"` on the root, and their own `<vision-page-bar>` swaps for a plain non-sticky `.embedded-toolbar` row via one shared `<ng-template>` + `NgTemplateOutlet` per page (`#deviceFiltersTpl`/`#deviceActionsTpl`, `#categoryFiltersTpl`/`#categoryActionsTpl`) so the embedded and standalone header markup can't drift. `inventory.html` passes `[embedded]="true"` to both mounts; Inventory's own `<vision-page-bar>` now hosts the Links/Categories tabs' own filters/actions inline (already true before this fix — those tabs render no filters into Inventory's `pageBarFilters` slot, so nothing else changed there).
  - **Categories' create/rename/set-connected now write** (`categories-logic.ts`/`categories-facade.ts`) — `POST`/`PUT /api/categories` (`isValidCategoryId`/`isValidCategoryName`/`canCreateCategory` gate the create form's submit client-side; the server's own `CategoryController#create` validates the identical kebab-case shape). The page's old "coming" notice (docs/plans/done/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md Wave 4) is deleted. Rename is a whole-record `PUT`, so it preserves the category's existing `attributeHints` (a field this form doesn't expose) rather than silently wiping it.
  - **Firmware/Hours now render real values (wave W9, docs/plans/active/WAREHOUSE-UX-CONTEXT.md "W8 → W9 handoff")** — W8 added `AssetSummaryResponse#firmware` (nullable `{name, version}`, joined at the vision-api read-model layer against `vision-flight`'s `vehicle_profiles`, never read by `vision-warehouse` itself) and `#totalFlightSeconds` to the one fleet-wide `GET /api/assets` response, closing the two backend discrepancies this bullet used to document (no fleet-wide firmware/hours source existed pre-W8; both the W3→W4 handoff's false claim and the never-flagged Hours gap are now moot). `vehicles-logic.ts#firmwareLabel(firmware)` renders `"<name> <version>"` (a small `FIRMWARE_NAME_LABELS` map spells out `ardupilot`/`px4`/`generic`; an unrecognized code renders verbatim rather than being swallowed), `'—'` only when both fields are absent — a real single-field or genuinely-zero-hours value (`formatFlightTime(0)` → `'0m'`) is never coerced to a dash. `formatFlightTime` is the pre-existing `core/fleet/asset-stats-logic.ts` duration formatter (asset-detail's own KPI band), reused rather than re-implemented.
  - **`effectiveRegistration` moved to `core/fleet/asset-attributes.ts`** (from `features/asset-detail/asset-detail-logic.ts`, which now just re-exports it for its own pre-existing import site) once this tab's own detail panel needed the identical `identity.registration`-falling-back-to-legacy-`attributes.registrationNumber` logic this wave found the detail panel was missing entirely (it briefly read `identity.registration` raw, no fallback, while the Asset Detail page's own equivalent field already had one) — closed before landing, both surfaces now read a pre-D1 asset's registration identically. Second-consumer-moves-to-`core/` again, same precedent as this bullet's own opening paragraph.
- **categories/** — mounted as Inventory's Categories tab (`/assets?tab=categories`, wave W4 — see the `inventory/` bullet above for the write-half `categories-logic.ts`/`categories-facade.ts` gained this wave: create/rename/set-connected, `POST`/`PUT /api/categories`).
- **devices/** — mounted as Inventory's Links tab (`/assets?tab=links`, wave W4 — see above); the page itself, its facade, and its own two-pane `?sel=<id>` list are otherwise unchanged.
  - **Devices' Source cell now names a shared endpoint instead of showing unrelated rows (docs/plans/active/OPERATOR-UX-7-PLAN.md finding D1, §2 D1, wave W3).** New pure `devices-page-logic.ts#endpointConflicts(devices)` → `Map<deviceId, string[]>` of the *other* ACTIVE (non-`DELETED`) device names sharing this one's exact `protocol`+`uri` (trailing-whitespace-only normalisation, no case-folding — the backend treats the pair as an opaque connection string). `DevicesFacade#conflictingEndpoints` feeds it `warehouseDevices()` (not the search-narrowed `warehouseRows()`, so typing in the search box never hides a device's own conflict fact); `DevicesPage#endpointConflictNames(device)` reads it. `devices.html`'s Source cell gained a second line, `Same endpoint as <names>`, in the plain warning-text register (new `.endpoint-conflict` class, `color: var(--color-warn-text)`, same secondary-line idiom as `features/system-status/system-status.css`'s `.stale-note`) — **not a chip**, since the row already carries its one state chip (frontend-style §5); `title` carries the untruncated list. No blocking, no backend change — a shared listener may be intentional.
  - **"Simulated" is now per-device, not per-asset-category, and the detail panel names each device's Sense/Sight role (docs/plans/active/SOURCE-ONBOARDING-2-PLAN.md D6/P3, wave W4 — full detail in the Status entry below).** `simulate-logic.ts#mapSimulatedDevices` reads `Device#origin === 'SIMULATED'` directly; `isSimulatedAsset`/`SIMULATED_CATEGORY` are deleted. The two-pane detail panel gained a "Role" fact row (`deviceRoleLabel`) and one more status chip (`deviceRoleStatus`/`roleStatusDescriptor`, both new in `devices-page-logic.ts`) alongside the existing merged state chip and "Simulated" chip; `DevicesFacade` gained `ownerDevices(assetId)`/`telemetryAgeMsFor(assetId)` (the latter off a new `fleetSummaryData` signal, `GET /api/fleet/summary`).
- **asset-detail/** (`/assets/:assetId` — the manager view, KPI stats fetched independently of `load()`, cockpit reached via `SettingsStore.flyAssetId` + `/fly`).
  - **Characteristics' Registration inline-edit is now a combined Identity editor (wave W4, docs/plans/active/WAREHOUSE-UX-PLAN.md §3.4)** — one `.inline-form.identity-edit` with Serial/Make/Model/Registration, `AssetDetailFacade#saveIdentity` → `asset-detail-logic.ts#buildIdentityEdit` → `PATCH .../identity` (a full-replacement `AssetIdentity`, same `AssetEdit#identity`'s own documented contract every other `AssetEdit` sub-object follows — a field left blank on save means "now unknown," never "leave alone"). Replaces the old single-field edit against `attributes['registrationNumber']` (`core/fleet/asset-attributes.ts`'s convention, now a read-only fallback via `effectiveRegistration()` — see the `inventory/` bullet above for where that function now lives). Read-only view shows Serial/Make/Model straight off `facade.identity()`, Registration off `facade.effectiveRegistration()`.
  - **KPI band gained a sixth tile, "Since service"** (`asset-detail-logic.ts#sinceServiceTile`) — composed from a new, independent `GET /api/assets/{id}/maintenance` fetch the facade kicks off alongside `load()`/`refresh()` (`AssetDetailFacade#loadMaintenanceRecords`, degrading to `[]` on any failure rather than blocking the page): `mostRecentlyClosedRecord` picks the latest-`closedAt` record, `hoursSinceClose` (`core/maintenance/maintenance-logic.ts`, wave W7, read-only cross-feature import — a generic, side-effect-free numeric helper with no coupling back to that wave's own page) turns it into an hour count, `humanAge` formats it as `"<age> ago"`. `'—'` for an asset with no closed maintenance record at all, never a fabricated "just serviced."
  - **Sense/Sight role status, two spots (docs/plans/active/SOURCE-ONBOARDING-2-PLAN.md P3, wave W4 — full detail in the Status entry below).** The cockpit-band header and the "Hardware & devices" subview's own section header both gained the same two chips, `Sense: …`/`Sight: …`, off new `AssetDetailFacade#senseStatus`/`sightStatus` computeds (`core/onboarding/fit-out-logic.ts#roleStatus`, frozen/consumed-only) and a new `telemetryAgeMs` computed reading a new `fleetSummaryData` signal (`GET /api/fleet/summary?includeArchived=true`, fetched via a new `loadFleetSummary()` wired into both `load()`/`refresh()`). `not-fitted` renders no chip at all (`AssetDetailPage#senseChip`/`sightChip` → `asset-detail-logic.ts#roleStatusDescriptor`, a deliberate duplicate of `devices-page-logic.ts`'s own identical mapping — see that Status entry for the "why duplicate, not shared" reasoning).
- **onboarding/** — rebuilt again in waves W2+W3 (docs/plans/active/SOURCE-ONBOARDING-2-PLAN.md §3.1, 2026-09-05) as **Source · Prove · Identify · Attach · Hand over**, five visible steps (`onboarding-logic.ts#WizardStep = 'source'|'prove'|'identify'|'attach'|'sysid'|'handover'`/`visibleSteps(rows, needsProve)`) plus the same hidden `sysid` interstitial that never appears in the rail. **The pre-W2 1161-line `onboarding.html` monolith is retired outright** into one 3-file component per step (`source-step.*`, `prove-step.*`, `identify-step.*`, `attach-step.*`, `sysid-step.*`, `handover-step.*`) plus the shared `vision-step-rail` (W1, `shared/ui/step-rail.*`) for the rail itself — `onboarding.ts`/`.html`/`.css` are now a thin shell: the rail (`OnboardingFacade#railItems`/`currentStepIndex`/`jumpTo` → `OnboardingStore#jumpToStep`, a one-way-door-past-creation guard the deliberately-unrestricted rail component itself doesn't enforce) plus a `@switch` delegating to each step's own component, plus one shared Back/Next footer for the three steps plain enough for it (`source`/`prove`/`identify`) — `attach`/`sysid`/`handover` each own a fully custom inline footer instead (their own action verbs don't fit a generic "Next"). Every step component injects `OnboardingFacade` directly — non-routed presentational children, licensed by `core/ui/architecture.spec.ts`'s own carve-out, same DI-sharing idiom `AssetDetailPage`'s `TelemetryStore`/`DetectionsStore` already established. A `connected: false` category still collapses the wizard to **Identify → Hand-over** only (WAREHOUSE-UX-PLAN.md D4 — equipment: a battery, a spare prop): Identify's own action button reads "Receive" (`OnboardingFacade#identifyActionLabel`, now rendered by the shell's shared footer, not the step itself) and calls `OnboardingStore#receiveEquipmentAsset()` directly, `POST /api/assets` with `devices` omitted.
  - **`source` is the new first step — the honest fork (D3)**, `source-step.*`: four tiles (`OnboardingFacade#sourceModeOptions`) — **"It comes to us"** (`passive` — the waiting room below, no picker, no Scan button, truly passive), **"We go to it"** (`manual` — the plain register-form for *both* rows immediately, no picker; fixes the pre-W3 defect where manual address entry only ever existed for Sight), **"Find it for me"** (`scan` — the five existing scanners, unchanged plumbing, `OnboardingFacade#rowFindMethods` narrows the tile grid to exclude `register` only under this mode so the register-form tile stays `manual`'s alone), **"Nothing to connect"** (`equipment` — forces both rows to `'none'`, `equipmentConfirmed: true`, same short-circuit as before, `OnboardingStore#chooseSourceMode`). A fifth tile, **"Provision Wi-Fi over USB"**, sits alongside the fork (U5/R3) — a plain nav tile to `/provision-wifi`, gated on `WebSerialGateway.isSupported()` but never hidden when unsupported, stating why it's inert instead (`OnboardingStore#provisionWifiSupported`). **The fork is a view-selector layered on top of the same two-row fit-out table** (`core/onboarding/fit-out-logic.ts`, unchanged, framework-free) — it decides which sub-UI an *unresolved* row renders, never overwriting a row already resolved (`OnboardingFacade#rowResolved`); a resolved row always renders as its own summary chip/card with a "Change" button regardless of the current mode. `scan` mode deliberately auto-selects nothing for either row (both still render their own tile grid) — an earlier draft auto-picked Sight's `discover` finder here, which was reverted because it made the legacy whole-vehicle Simulate demo path (the tile grid's own third, non-`FIT_OUT_FIND_METHODS` "Use a test source" tile) unreachable for Sight under both `manual` and `scan`.
  - **Candidate entrance** (`?candidateId=<uuid>`, `OnboardingStore#applyCandidateQueryPrefill`/`prefillFromDiscoveryCandidate`) — prefills one fit-out row as already-filled-and-proven (`preProvenRoles`, role derived from `DiscoveryCandidate.method`: `mavlink` → Sense, else → Sight), the other row stays `—`; `sourceMode` is set to `passive`. A pre-proven role is excluded from `rowsNeedProve`/`canAdvanceProve` (`OnboardingStore`'s own *effective* versions of `fit-out-logic.ts#needsProve`/`canAdvanceFromFitOutProve`, which don't know about pre-proven rows) — but the Hand-over terminal proof still renders it honestly as "not yet verified" until an actual probe has run (`sightTerminalProof`/`senseTerminalProof` never read `preProvenRoles`, only a real `lastProbeResult`/`lastVerifyResult`). Any manual edit to a pre-proven row's connection fields un-trusts the claim (`clearPreProven`, called from every row mutator).
  - **The waiting room** (`source-step.html`, `passive` mode, unresolved rows only) — live per-row state from `core/onboarding/intake-logic.ts#intakeState` (frozen, pure), polled via a new `PollScheduler` registration (`OnboardingStore#DISCOVERY_STATUS_POLL_MS = 3_000` — snappier than `DiscoveryInboxStore`'s 30s inbox-sweep floor since this screen is actively watched) hitting `VisionApi#discoveryStatus()`; `OnboardingFacade#intakeFor(role)` layers the store's own recorded Prove-step probe result over the raw read so a row already tested reads `proven`, never fabricating that state itself. A `heard` candidate (not yet used) shows a live "Use" button (`OnboardingFacade#heardCandidateFor` → `OnboardingStore#useHeardCandidate`, same prefill path as the candidate-entrance above). The Sight row also renders a **push-address card** (`onboarding-logic.ts#composePushAddress`, `rtsp://<LAN address>:<videoPushPort>/<videoPushPathPrefix><name>` — omitted entirely, with an honest reason, if either optional wire field is absent, never a fabricated host) and the Sense row renders a collapsible **P1 diagnostic panel** (raw `DiscoveryStatusResponse` facts: bound/bindAddress/lobbyHeld/datagramsReceived/bytesReceived/lastDatagramAt, unclaimed/claimed sysids, push port/path prefix/ready paths, per-source health including `NEVER_SCANNED` → "Never scanned" — never rendering a fabricated "broken").
  - **Prove is unchanged in substance** (still `OnboardingStore#proveByRole: Record<FitOutRole, RowProveState>`, `prove-step.*`) but now **excludes any `preProvenRoles` row** from both its own step-visibility (`rowsNeedProve`) and its row loop — re-proving a row the operator just watched arrive live in the waiting room would be busywork, not honesty.
  - **`identify` moved after `source`/`prove`** (was the first step) — same fields (name/category/photo/serial/make/model), same equipment short-circuit tell, but the category picker is now narrowed on the equipment path (`OnboardingStore#identifyCategoryOptions`, `equipment()` = `isEquipmentPath(rows)` off the `source` step's own fork choice, not a category fact chosen *after* Connect the way the pre-W2 wizard worked) to `connected: false` categories only — an operator who already said "nothing to connect" is never then offered a vehicle category.
  - **`attach` (was `register`) gained a new-vs-existing-asset fork** (§0.2, `attach-step.*`) — **"New vehicle"** is unchanged in substance (`buildCreateAssetRequest(identify, rows)` → `POST /api/assets`); **"Add to an existing asset"** (`OnboardingStore#attachTarget`/`existingAssets`/`chooseAttachTarget`/`selectExistingAsset`) attaches this visit's connection onto an already-registered asset instead (`GET /api/assets` for the picker, then either the atomic candidate-attach call `DiscoveryInboxStore#attachCandidate` when this visit started from a discovery candidate, or a plain register-device-then-`assignDevice` otherwise — `OnboardingStore#attachToExistingAsset`/`registerAndAssignRows`). Sets `existingAssetPath`, read by Hand-over to skip its own custodian picker (that asset already has an owner).
  - **`sysid` (FLEET-RADIO-PLAN.md R5/F0) unchanged in substance** — `combinedSysidCollision({sense, sight})` (Sense checked first — only a `mavlink` link ever collides) feeds the same collision-fix interstitial (`sysid-collision-logic.ts#detectSysidCollision`/`#sysidParameterName`, `VisionApi#writeAssetParameter`); every other path (no collision, the equipment short-circuit, the legacy Simulate path, or `existingAssetPath`) skips straight to Hand-over. "Continue" always proceeds regardless of write outcome.
  - **Hand-over now always ends in a two-half terminal proof, never a single `handoverNext()` router-link guess (D9)** — `handoverNext()` is deleted outright. `handover-step.html` renders `OnboardingFacade#sightProof()`/`senseProof()` (`onboarding-logic.ts#sightTerminalProof`/`senseTerminalProof`, frozen/pure — fed the Prove step's own recorded `lastProbeResult`/`lastVerifyResult`) side by side: a `find` row that actually passed its Test/Verify renders `proven` with a short evidence string ("first frame 1920×1080", "heartbeat, sysid 4 — ArduCopter"); a `none`/`simulate`/pre-proven-but-never-actually-tested row renders honestly unproven ("—", "simulated, not yet verified", "not yet verified") — never a fabricated green tick. Below both halves, an **"Open cockpit ›"** link routes to `/fly/:assetId?autostart=1` (the cockpit-side consumption of that query param is wave W5's own job, not this one's). The **"Issue to"/"Leave in stock" picker still runs first for the new-asset path** exactly as before (`OnboardingStore#issueToCustodian`/`leaveInStock`, same `setAssetCustody`+`assignPilot` two-call shape, same custodian-candidate roster reuse, same dev-parity gotcha below) — `existingAssetPath` skips it outright (`enterHandoverStep` returns before the roster fetch), landing straight on the terminal proof with an "Attached onto "<name>"." line instead of an issue/stock outcome banner.
  - **Entry-point prefill contracts** — `/add-source?deviceId=<uuid>` (`OnboardingStore#applyDevicePrefill`, unchanged from pre-W2: `roleForDevice`, silent-degrade, the same "creates a new device row, not a reference by id" known limitation) and the new `/add-source?candidateId=<uuid>` above. Both silent-degrade identically (unknown id, 403, or no param at all → both rows stay empty).
  - **Registration now rides `identity.registration`, not the `attributes['registrationNumber']` convention** (`onboarding-logic.ts#buildIdentityRequest`) — `core/fleet/asset-attributes.ts`'s attribute-key convention predates `dto.IdentityRequest`'s first-class `registration` column; wave W4 later gave `features/asset-detail/**`'s own inline edit and the Inventory page's own detail panel the identical `identity`-first behavior (see the `inventory/`/`asset-detail/` bullets above) — every read/write path in the app now agrees. **The persistence discrepancy this bullet used to flag here is closed**: the V28 migration's backfill originally read `attributes ->> 'registration'`, but the frontend's actual historical convention wrote `'registrationNumber'`, so the backfill silently did nothing for real historical data — fixed in commit `280b208e` (`fix(persistence): V28 backfills registration from the historical registrationNumber key`, landed alongside W4), which now coalesces both keys before dropping them.
  - `features/assets/assets-facade.ts`'s pre-wizard single-device empty-state quick-add ("Add the simulated source", bypassing this wizard entirely) **moved to `InventoryFacade#registerSimulator`** when that page was deleted this wave (W4) — same one-click behavior, same "no hardware needed" empty-state button, just relocated onto the Vehicles/Equipment tabs' own empty state (`vehicles-table.html`). `features/devices/**`'s own empty state carries no equivalent bypass — it only ever pointed at "+ Add source" above, unchanged. **Re-checked in wave W9**, which was asked to delete this quick-add "if it still exists" on `assets-facade.ts`/`devices-facade.ts`: `features/assets/**` no longer exists at all (deleted outright by W4, confirmed above), and `devices-facade.ts` has no quick-add of any kind — only `goToAddSource()`, which navigates to the wizard. `InventoryFacade#registerSimulator` (a *different* facade than either named target) is this same relocation, already the accepted end state per this bullet's own W4 history, not a second bypass to remove. No code change was needed for this item.
- **provisioning/** (`/provision-wifi`, `orgGuard` — same gate as `/add-source`, docs/plans/active/ZERO-CONFIG-ONBOARDING-CONTEXT.md §4/§8, wave Z5) — `ProvisioningPage`/`ProvisioningFacade`: Wi-Fi-over-USB device provisioning via the open Improv Serial standard (https://www.improv-wifi.com/serial/), reached from `onboarding.html`'s drone-config step ("Provision Wi-Fi over USB" link in the `@case ('config')` block) as an alternative to copying MAVLink connection strings by hand. **Pure frontend — no `VisionApi` call anywhere in this feature**; the device is never told this platform's own address, here or anywhere else, closing §2's research finding (the device learns only Wi-Fi credentials over USB, connects on its own, and announces itself — the existing discovery inbox picks it up from there).
  - `improv-serial-protocol.ts` (framework-free, no DOM dependency) — the wire codec: `"IMPROV"` magic + version + type + length + data + checksum framing, `ImprovPacketType`/`ImprovCurrentState`/`ImprovErrorState`/`ImprovRpcCommand` enums, `buildImprovFrame`/`buildRpcCommandFrame`/`buildRequestCurrentStateFrame`/`buildSendWifiSettingsFrame` (write side), `decodeImprovFrame` (one complete frame) + `readImprovBytes` (a pure `(state, chunk) => step` incremental byte-stream reducer, `player-recovery.ts`-shaped, that resyncs a sliding window past noise and never wedges on a bad-checksum frame — device chatter before Improv starts is expected, not fatal). Verified against the reference `improv-wifi/sdk-serial-js` source directly (not just the protocol page's own summary table, which omits `NOT_AUTHORIZED` — kept here anyway, decodable, documented BLE-only). `improvCurrentStateLabel`/`improvErrorMessage` never fabricate: an unrecognized code names its own raw value (CLAUDE.md "degrade honestly").
  - `web-serial-gateway.ts` — the **only** file in this feature that touches `navigator.serial` (not in this project's TS DOM lib — Web Serial is Chromium-only and never shipped a standard lib entry; minimal ambient interfaces declared locally, not as a global augmentation). `WebSerialGateway` (`providedIn: 'root'`): `isSupported()` (checked live — `'serial' in navigator && window.isSecureContext`, so a reload in a different browser/context reflects reality, never cached) and `requestConnection(): Promise<ImprovSerialConnection>` (opens the browser's own device picker, then the port at Improv's fixed 115200 baud). `ImprovSerialConnection` is the narrow interface (`onData`/`onDisconnect`/`write`/`close`) `ProvisioningFacade` depends on — a test swaps in a fake wholesale (`{ provide: WebSerialGateway, useValue: fakeGateway }`), so nothing in this feature's specs needs real hardware, a secure context, or a Chromium browser. `NavigatorSerialConnection`'s `close()` is idempotent (a `closing` flag distinguishes an explicit Disconnect from a real unplug, so `onDisconnect` never fires for a caller's own close) and releases the reader/writer/port cleanly.
  - `provisioning-logic.ts` (+spec, pure) — `canSubmitWifiSettings` (session open + device `READY` + non-blank SSID + not already sending; password is optional, an open network is real), `deviceStateChipTone`, `describeSerialRequestError` (DOMException name → honest copy: `NotFoundError`/`SecurityError`/`NetworkError`/`InvalidStateError`, never a bare "Error"), `isCancelledPickerError`.
  - `provisioning-facade.ts` (`@Injectable()`, page-local, no store — same `PreflightFacade`/`ModelsFacade` shape) — three independently-changing state layers: the USB link's own lifecycle (`session`: idle/connecting/open/link-lost/request-failed), what the device last reported (`deviceState`/`deviceError`, decoded from `WebSerialGateway`'s byte stream), and the form/RPC state (`ssid`/`password`/`sending`/`redirectUrl`) — **never persisted**, plain signals, no `localStorage` call anywhere in this file. A `HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS` (3s) bounds "waiting for the device's first frame" so a non-Improv device reads as a stalled handshake, not an infinite spinner. `DestroyRef.onDestroy()` releases the reader/writer/port on component destroy, same as an explicit Disconnect.
  - `ProvisioningPage` injects only `ProvisioningFacade` (`core/ui/architecture.spec.ts`'s `ROUTED_PAGES` guard). Capability check first: no Web Serial/insecure context → `vision-empty` explainer card, never a broken button. Connected but no credentials sent yet → SSID + password form (reveal toggle, `type="password"`⇄`"text"`, no icon — `eye-off` isn't in the frozen `IconName` set, so this uses a plain "Show"/"Hide" text button instead of extending it). A device-unplug mid-flow renders the same `link-lost` session state as a `Disconnect` click never would (a distinct `onDisconnect` callback, not inferred from a write failure). Success shows the device's own redirect URL when it sent one (never a fabricated one); failure names the Improv error code's own meaning via `improvErrorMessage`. Daylight-chart styling throughout — plain `.card`/`.field`/`.chip`/`.btn`/`vision-notice`/`vision-empty` tokens, no `.surface-dark` (a bench utility, not a cockpit).
- **replay/** — `ReplayLibraryPage` (`/replay`) and `ReplayPage` (routed, *and* embedded non-routed for the `?asset=&usage=&t=` deep link); `AfterActionPanel` renders all six evidence parts always, in order.
  - **`ReplayPage` leads with the replay, not the evidence package (docs/plans/active/OPERATOR-UX-6-PLAN.md finding E1, §2 E1, wave W1).** `replay.html`'s `.detail-grid` order is now: **Position** (map) + **Recording** (video) side by side, `span-1` each (new `.span-1` class in `replay.css`, stacked full-width under 900px, same breakpoint as `.span-2`) → **Playback** (scrub/transport, `span-2`) → Telemetry-at-scrub / Detections-at-scrub → **Evidence package** last, `span-2`. `AfterActionPanel` itself now owns a `collapsed = signal(true)` (default collapsed, plain component-local state — no host reacts to it, mirrors `shared/ui/preflight-checklist.ts`'s `collapsed` idiom but as an internal signal, not an `input`/`output` pair) — collapsed, only its header line (title + `Complete` chip + "Download package") renders; the part-list/caveats body is behind the `.disclosure` toggle button (chevron icon, `after-action-panel.css`'s new `.disclosure`/`.header-actions` rules). The mid-flight mount (`replay.html`'s `usageOpen` branch) is unaffected — same component, same default.
  - **Actor ids read as names (finding E2, §2 E2).** `AfterActionPanel#scopedToLabel` reuses `core/audit/summary-logic.ts#actorLabel` (root principal → `Station`, else an 8-char short id) instead of interpolating `manifest.scopedTo`'s raw UUID — `Built for Station` rather than `Built for 00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000`. `ReplayFacade` holds no user roster (no other reader on this page needs one), so this passes an empty names map; `actorLabel` already degrades an unresolved id to its own short-id fallback, the same "no roster known" posture every other reader of that function without a roster gets.
  - **One `pluralize`, explicit plurals (finding E3, §2 E3).** New `shared/ui/text-logic.ts#pluralize(count, singular, plural?)` (+ spec) is the canonical import for this feature going forward — `replay.ts`/`replay-library.ts`/`after-action-panel.ts` all now import it instead of `shared/ui/page-bar/page-bar.ts`'s identical export (left as-is, out of this wave's file scope, still used elsewhere). The actual E3 bug — `after-action-panel.ts`'s `passport`/`audit` rows reading `"Included · 200 entrys"` — was a missing explicit plural at the call site, not a defect in `pluralize` itself: `PART_COUNT_NOUN` is now `Record<AfterActionPart, readonly [singular, plural]>` (`['entry', 'entries']` for both), so `countLabel` always passes both forms.
  - **Vehicle-neutral copy on `ReplayPage` (finding E4, §2 E4).** Operator-visible "flight" wording on this one page → "session": page-bar title `Flight replay` → `Replay`; the time-range line's `(N flight)` → `(N session)`; the open-usage empty state's `This flight is still in progress` → `This session is still in progress` (+ its body copy); "No position/recording/detections … for this flight" → "…this session"; "clip covers the whole flight" → "…whole session". **Left un-fixed, out of file scope:** `core/after-action/after-action-logic.ts#openFlightNotice` still literally renders `"This flight is still in progress — the package covers everything recorded up to now."` inside the evidence panel's own `openNotice` — a second instance of the exact wording E4 flagged, sourced from `core/`, not `features/replay/**`, so out of this wave's file scope; a future `core/after-action/**` touch should rename it (and its `AfterActionView#openNotice`/tests) to match. The plan's own E4 design note ("the replay library already says session") does not match the code — `replay-library.html`/`.ts`/`replay-library-logic.ts` still say "flight" throughout (`countNoun="flight"`, `No flights match these filters`, etc.) — untouched here since finding E4's own examples are all on `ReplayPage`, not the library list; flagged for whoever picks up the library's own copy next.
  - **An open usage (no `endedAt`) no longer claims `Flying now` once its last activity has gone stale (docs/plans/active/OPERATOR-UX-5-PLAN.md finding U1, §2 U1, wave W2).** `replay-library-logic.ts#usageStatus(usage, nowMs)` is the one place this list and its detail pane derive a row's status: `sampleCount === 0` wins outright (`No samples`, not a replay link, sorted last — `sortUsagesForDisplay`) even over an open usage; otherwise an open usage is `Flying now` (green, live dot) only while its last-activity estimate is within `OPEN_USAGE_STALE_AFTER_SECONDS` (10 minutes — a reasoned placeholder citing the backend's `vision.usage.idle-close` default, not a measured constant, same posture as `flight-state-logic.ts`'s own documented placeholder thresholds), else a neutral `Open · last sample 4d 2h ago` chip (no live dot) via `humanAge`. **The wire carries no last-activity timestamp for a usage** (`UsageSummary` has `startedAt`/`endedAt?`/`durationSeconds?`/`sampleCount` only) — `usageStatus` estimates it as `startedAt + durationSeconds` when `durationSeconds` is present, else bare `startedAt`; since `DefaultUsageService` (Java, grep-confirmed) never populates `durationSeconds` while `endedAt` is null, this degrades today to plain `startedAt`, documented in the function's own comment as a known limitation pending a real last-sample timestamp on the DTO. `replay-library.html`'s table Duration cell and the detail pane's chips/actions both branch on `usageStatus(usage).kind`/`tone`/`live`/`isReplayLink` — a `no-samples` row's detail pane omits the "Open replay ›" action entirely rather than link to a usage with nothing to scrub.
- **controller/** (`/manage/controller`) — `ControllerSetupPage`/`ControllerSetupFacade` rebuilt in wave X4 (docs/plans/active/CONTROLLER-UX-PLAN.md §2.3) as a step-by-step setup wizard over a flat editor, both views over the same `ProfileDraft`:
  - **Layout bar** (the page's own template, replacing the old two-column "Layouts" list) — one `<select>` of `facade.profiles()`, kind/Built-in/Active chips, an Activate button when the selected profile isn't active, a "New layout" inline disclosure (kind + name, starts as a copy of the kind's built-in), an "All controls ▾" toggle, and Delete (+ `ConfirmDialog`) for a `SAVED` profile. The two disclosures share one **second** `UiStore` group (`panels`, separate from the page's pre-existing `dialog` confirm group — `UiStore`'s own "overlays that may legitimately overlap get separate instances" carve-out: a delete confirm can be open at the same time as the flat editor, just not both disclosures at once).
  - **`StepRail`** (`<vision-step-rail>`, `step-rail.*`) — renders `wizardSteps(kind, builtInForKind, catalog)` from `core/rc/controller-wizard-logic.ts` as clickable chips, `stepStatus(step, draft)` driving done (check icon)/current (this app's one selection language: inset bar + `--color-info-soft`)/open — always read off the draft itself, never separate wizard state, so the rail can never disagree with what is actually bound.
  - **`WizardStep`** (`<vision-wizard-step>`, `wizard-step.*`) — one step's two-card body. Left: instruction + live detection — accumulates every axis/button reading via `observeInputs`/`detectedControl` while the step is open (decision U9: detection is always-on, no separate Detect button) and shows a vertical gauge (`CHANNEL`) or `SwitchGauge` (`ARM`/`MODE`/`EXTRAS`); `connected() === false` swaps this for a small manual source+index picker (item 7 — the wizard stays usable with no transmitter plugged in). Right: `CHANNEL` direction/rest tiles + an `Advanced` disclosure for channel/µs (`controller-setup-logic.ts#microsFor`, exported this wave); `ARM`/`MODE`/`EXTRAS` per-position action selects narrowed to that step's own family (`controller-wizard-logic.ts#actionsForStep`) with a `MODE_NAME` datalist fed by `ControllerSetupFacade#knownModeNames` (CONTROLLER-UX-PLAN.md §5 wave M — the union of `selectableModes` from every currently-online asset whose `vehicleKind` matches the layout's own kind, via the same `VisionApi#flightCapabilities` call the cockpit's command panel already makes, just fanned out over every `STREAMING` asset instead of one; free text always stays the fallback, with a quiet "Not a mode this vehicle reported" note — never blocking — when the typed value isn't among them and an honest `modeSourceHint` line ("Names from 2 online vehicles" / "No online vehicle to read mode names from — type one, e.g. LOITER") under the shared datalist) or an `AUX_FUNCTION` select, a dangerous-action note, and (EXTRAS only) an "Add another"/existing-rows list (`actionStepKindOf` classifies a bound `ACTIONS` control as `ARM`/`MODE`/`EXTRAS` from what its positions actually fire); `REVIEW` renders `<vision-transmitter-view>` driven by `toUpdateRequest(draft)`, `reviewChecks`, and `facade.issues()`, with Save/Save-and-activate. **U12 (built-in read-only):** `editable() === false` replaces the right card with one "Make a copy to edit" notice+button for every step kind; Next still advances (`onNext()` skips committing when not editable) so read-only browsing of a built-in's own steps still works.
  - **`AllControls`** (`<vision-all-controls>`, `all-controls.*`) — the pre-X4 flat editor (live transmitter inventory + Detect gesture, one control-card per bound row, all fields at once), moved verbatim behind the "All controls" toggle for an operator who already knows their transmitter or needs a control the fixed step list doesn't visit. Injects `ControllerSetupFacade`/`RcInputService` directly rather than taking them as inputs — both are provided on `ControllerSetupPage` and this only ever renders inside that page's template, so the same singletons resolve via ordinary hierarchical DI; `core/ui/architecture.spec.ts`'s injection guard only scans the `ROUTED_PAGES` list's own file, not its children, so this doesn't weaken it.
  - **`ControllerSetupFacade#replaceDraft(draft)`** (new this wave) — the wizard's write path: replaces the whole draft in one shot with what `controller-wizard-logic.ts`'s pure `applyChannelStep`/`applyActionStep` computed, rather than chaining the facade's existing per-field setters (`setKind`/`setRole`/…) one at a time. Chosen because those two functions keep invariants the setters can't reproduce by chaining — `applyChannelStep` *drops* a different control's now-superseded binding of the same function entirely (no "unbind this other control" setter exists), and both functions replace a re-roled control's `positions` outright, where `setRole` leaves the previous role's positions sitting there.
  - `core/rc/controller-wizard-logic.ts` gained `actionStepKindOf` and `actionsForStep` (+ an `ARM_STEP_ACTIONS` family — `ARM_ACTIONS` widened with `DISARM` — kept separate from `ARM_ACTIONS` so `stepStatus`/`reviewChecks`' "is this actually armed" check is untouched); `core/rc/controller-setup-logic.ts` exported `MAX_RC_CHANNEL` and `microsFor`; `core/rc/control-surface-logic.ts` exported `displayPercentFor` (the same pad-fill math, usable before a real `ManualControlChannelBinding` exists).
  - **`ControllerSetupFacade#knownModeNames`** (CONTROLLER-UX-PLAN.md §5 wave M) — one read-only `computed<KnownModeNames>` (`core/rc/controller-wizard-logic.ts`'s own exported type: `{ names: readonly string[]; assetCount: number }`), bundled rather than split into two facade signals so `modeSourceHint`'s "Names from N online vehicles" copy can never count assets and names out of step with each other. Fed by `loadKnownModeCapabilities()` — a lazy, fire-and-forget read kicked off once from `load()` (so it never blocks the page or the profile/catalogue load it rides alongside): `listAssets()` filtered to `status === 'STREAMING'` ("online", the same vocabulary `drone-picker.ts` already uses), then `flightCapabilities(assetId)` — the identical per-asset call `features/fly/cockpit-facade.ts#loadCapabilities` already makes for the cockpit's own command panel — fanned out over every online asset via `Promise.allSettled`; a failed list or a failed single asset read is logged and skipped, never toasted, never blocking (no fleet-wide capabilities store existed to reuse, so this is a new but narrowly-scoped fetch, not a new endpoint). `core/rc/controller-wizard-logic.ts` gained the pure `modeNamesFor(kind, capabilities)` (de-duplicated union of `selectableModes` for capabilities whose `vehicleKind` matches, first-seen order) and `modeSourceHint(count, assetCount)` (the honest copy above; `count === 0` — no names, whether from no online match or an online match that reported none — always reads as "type one yourself", never a technically-true but useless "from 1 vehicle" naming nothing).
- **vision-profiles/** (`/vision/profiles`, docs/plans/active/CV-SETTINGS-PLAN.md §4/§5, wave W6) — **Profiles**, the CV config hierarchy's own editor: named `CvProfile`s (model, confidence, fps, allow/deny label lists, detection on/off, tracking, an event-open/close rule) bound per ORGANIZATION/CATEGORY/ASSET, plus a fleet-wide coverage table. `VisionProfilesPage`/`VisionProfilesFacade` (page-local facade, no store — same precedent as `MaintenanceFacade`/`ModelsFacade`) load everything once (`Promise.all` over `getCvProfiles`/`getCvModels`/`getCvTrackers`/`listCategories`/`listAssets`/`listAssetGroups`/`getCvCoverage`) and degrade the whole page to one error notice on any failure (CLAUDE.md "degrade honestly" — a profiles/bindings page has no meaningful partial-content state; every section reads off the same load). Replaces `features/settings/detection-settings.*` outright (deleted this wave, not repointed) — the old page only ever edited one global default; this one is the real per-scope hierarchy PLATFORM→ORGANIZATION→CATEGORY→ASSET resolves against at stream start (unchanged CV-SETTINGS-PLAN.md rule: **resolved once at stream start, never mid-flight** — the page states this once in its own page-bar hint, "Profiles apply when a stream starts; a running stream keeps its config"). Route-guarded `orgGuard` (exactly `canManageOrg` at the route level — confirmed by reading `core/org/org-guard.ts`), and the rail's own **Profiles** entry (`nav-entries.ts`, VISION group, `managerOnly: true`) — so a pilot never reaches this page at all, matching the plan's "write gates canManageOrg" instruction read together with the task's explicit route-guard requirement.
  - **Pure logic** — `vision-profiles-logic.ts` (+ spec): `sortProfilesForDisplay` (built-in first, alphabetical), row gates `canEditProfile`/`canForkProfile`/`canDeleteProfile` (a built-in profile can only be forked, never edited/deleted), `ProfileDraft` + `emptyProfileDraft`/`draftFromProfile`/`forkDraftFromProfile`/`forkedProfileName` ("Copy of X", "Copy of X (2)", …), `parseLabelList`/`formatLabelList` (comma/newline-separated allow/deny textareas ↔ `readonly string[]`), `validateDraft` (name/model required, confidence 0–1, fps ≥1, tracking fields ≥0 only when `mode !== 'OFF'`), `draftToRequest`, `isModelMissingOnWorker` (a profile referencing a model whose `availability !== 'PRESENT'` degrades to a visible "not on this worker" notice, never a silent no-op). Coverage: `summarizeProfileBindings`/`bindingSummaryLabel`/`describeCoverageSource`/`describeCoverageFilters`/`coverageRowClearTarget`.
  - **No `GET /api/cv/bindings` endpoint exists in the frozen wire contract** — "what's bound to profile X" is derived client-side from `GET /api/cv/coverage` instead (`summarizeProfileBindings`, grouped by `profileId`), not a second read. Per-row Clear on the coverage table only targets `CATEGORY`/`ASSET` sources (`coverageRowClearTarget` returns `null` for `ORGANIZATION`/`PLATFORM` by design — clearing an org default from one fleet-wide row would have organization-wide blast radius); a separate "Clear organization default" button in the Bindings panel handles that case explicitly, targeting the caller's own primary group (`primaryGroupId(memberships)` — a documented single-org simplification, since `MeResponse` carries a membership list but this UI has no multi-org switcher anywhere).
  - **Widened `CvModel`** (`core/api/models.ts`) with optional `version`/`taskType`/`runtime`/`classes`/`status`/`availability`/`metrics`/`provenance`/`source` — every new field is `?:` so the pre-existing `features/fly/cv-control-panel-logic.spec.ts` object-literal fixture (out of this wave's file scope) still compiles unchanged. `TrainingRun`/`TrainingRunsResponse` types added for W8's own future use, not yet consumed by any component this wave.
  - **`VisionApi`** gained 8 methods this wave: `getCvProfiles`/`createCvProfile`/`updateCvProfile`/`deleteCvProfile`, `setCvProfileBinding`/`deleteCvProfileBinding` (`DELETE` with a JSON body, `{body: {...}}` option — same idiom other body-carrying deletes in this file use), `getEffectiveCvProfile`, `getCvCoverage`. The registry promote/rollback/training-runs endpoints from the same plan section are explicitly **not** implemented here — CV-SETTINGS-PLAN.md scopes those to wave W8.
  - **Backend does not exist yet** — every method above is coded against the frozen §5 wire contract's JSON shapes exactly (field names, wrapped-list responses `{profiles:[...]}`/`{rows:[...]}`), not against a live server. A 404/500 from any of these calls today degrades through the page's ordinary `describeHttpError` error notice, same as any other failed read/write.
- **labeling/, models/, training-jobs/** (`/manage/training`, `/manage/training/models`, `/manage/training/jobs/:jobId`, `/manage/training/runs[/:runId]`, docs/plans/active/CV-SETTINGS-PLAN.md §3.2/§3.3/§4/§5.2/§6, wave W8) — the CV-training loop's three route trees, now cross-linked by one shared **`<vision-cv-subnav>`** (`shared/ui/cv-subnav.*`, `active: 'models'|'labeling'|'training'`) atop each tree's own root page (`ModelsPage`, `DatasetsPage`, the new `RunHistoryPage`) so a dataset → its run → its produced model is walkable without returning to the rail. Deliberately **not** added to any drill-in page (`DatasetDetailPage`, `SampleEditorPage`, `TrainingJobPage`, the new `RunDetailPage`) — those already carry their own "Back to …" breadcrumbs/cross-links, so a second always-visible nav row there would just repeat one. `active` is an explicit input, not `routerLinkActive` — `/manage/training` (Labeling), `/manage/training/models`, and `/manage/training/runs` all share the literal string prefix `/manage/training`, so a non-exact `routerLinkActive` would highlight the wrong tab. **Vision rail entries are unchanged** (`nav-entries.ts`, untouched) — this is a page-level lateral jump between the three trees, not a rail change.
  - **Models (`ModelsPage`/`ModelsFacade`, `/manage/training/models`) rewritten from its worker-only shape** (docs/plans/done/CV-TRAINING-PLAN.md Phase 2 T10) into the joined registry read model §3.2 describes: worker truth (`ModelInfo`) merged with platform governance — status (DRAFT/CANDIDATE/LIVE/RETIRED, one chip), runtime (PYTORCH/OPENVINO, muted text), availability (`MISSING_ON_WORKER` an explicit `.availability-warn` line, never hidden), metrics labelled **"Training mAP50"** (never presented as a held-out evaluation), provenance (dataset→run links when present). `source: 'config'` (registry unreachable/off) renders every row anyway plus one `vision-notice` saying so — `GET /api/cv/models` never errors server-side by contract, so this page has no "disabled" empty state any more. **Promote** (`VisionApi#promoteModel`, widened to return `PromotionResultResponse{id,version,status,previousModelId?,previousVersion?}`) and a new page-level **Roll back** (`VisionApi#rollbackModel`, `POST /api/cv/registry/rollback`) both gate on `models-logic.ts#canAdministerRegistry` — **`ModelRegistryController#promote`/`#rollback` require `canAdminister()` (ADMIN/unbounded scope only)**, narrower than the route's own `orgGuard` (`canManageOrg`, ADMIN|MANAGER) — a MANAGER can see the registry but never mutate it; this predicate is file-local (`models-logic.ts`, not `core/org/org-logic.ts`, out of this wave's write scope) since it answers a materially different question than `canManageOrg`. Roll back requires an explicit confirm (`rollbackConfirmMessage`) naming the model it retires — never a fabricated guess at which model gets restored, since the wire gives no way to know that ahead of the call; a `409` ("nothing to roll back to") reads as its own honest notice, not a raw error. Dead code deleted: `RegisteredModel`/`RegisteredModelsResponse` (`core/api/models.ts`) and `VisionApi#registryModels` — the last caller, `DatasetDetailFacade#loadBaseModelOptions`, now calls `getCvModels()` like everything else.
  - **Labeling (`DatasetsPage`) unchanged except the new sub-nav** — its old header-actions "Models" link is gone, replaced outright by `<vision-cv-subnav active="labeling">` (always visible, not gated by `!disabled()` like the old link was — jumping to another CV-settings tree is meaningful even when this deployment's own training feature is off).
  - **Training (new `RunHistoryPage`/`RunDetailPage`, `features/training-jobs/**`) is the persisted run history**, distinct from the pre-existing `TrainingJobPage`'s live in-flight poll view (`TrainingRun` vs. `TrainingJobResponse` — see `core/api/models.ts#TrainingRun`'s own doc comment for the split; a run still in progress is watched live at `/manage/training/jobs/:jobId`, not here — this page never polls). `VisionApi#getTrainingRuns()`/`#getTrainingRun(runId)` (`GET /api/cv/training/runs[/:runId]`, new this wave) are `canManageOrg`-gated server-side (`TrainingJobService#runs`/`#run`) — both new routes carry `orgGuard`, added to `training-jobs.routes.ts`'s existing `TRAINING_JOB_ROUTES` array **ahead of `LABELING_ROUTES`** (already true before this wave) since `manage/training/runs` is a static 3-segment path that would otherwise be swallowed by `LABELING_ROUTES`'s `manage/training/:datasetId` param route — the identical hazard `MODELS_ROUTES`'s own doc comment documents for `manage/training/models`; `manage/training/runs/:runId` (4 segments) faces no such hazard. `RunHistoryPage` lists every run newest-first (`run-history-logic.ts#sortRunsNewestFirst`) as full-row links (state chip, progress, loss, mAP50, dataset name, started-by/at) — one large touch target per row rather than a table + trailing action column, the same idiom `dataset-detail.html`'s own "Training jobs" list already uses. `RunDetailPage` is the dataset → run → produced model walk's middle link: a "Dataset" action link back to `DatasetDetailPage`, and — once `hasProducedModel(run)` — an "open the model registry" link forward into `ModelsPage` (whose own Provenance column links back here from the model side, `['/manage/training/runs', runId]`). Both pages degrade honestly: a `404` (unknown run, or `vision.training.enabled=false`) renders `vision-empty`, never a blocked page; a `RUNNING` run with no produced model yet reads a quiet "still training" notice rather than linking to `/manage/training/jobs/:runId` — nothing on the wire guarantees `TrainingRun#runId` equals `TrainingJobResponse#jobId`, so that link is deliberately not fabricated.
  - **`core/api/vision-api.ts`** gained `getTrainingRuns()`/`getTrainingRun(runId)`; `promoteModel` widened to `Promise<PromotionResultResponse>`; new `rollbackModel()`. **`core/api/models.ts`**: `CvModel.status` corrected to the real `'DRAFT'|'CANDIDATE'|'LIVE'|'RETIRED'` union (was `'LIVE'|'CANDIDATE'|'ARCHIVED'`, a W6-era guess the real backend DTOs — read directly off the concurrent W5 Java agent's uncommitted `CvModelResponse.java` on this same shared tree — never actually send); new `PromotionResultResponse`; `RegisteredModel`/`RegisteredModelsResponse` deleted.
  - **Pure logic**: `models-logic.ts` (+spec) rewritten wholesale — `modelKey`, `canAdministerRegistry`, `canPromoteModel`, `resolvePromoteVersion`, `sortModelsForDisplay`, `statusLabel`/`statusChipVariant`, `runtimeLabel`, `isMissingOnWorker`, `metricsKindLabel`/`formatMap50`, `hasProvenance`, `catalogSource`, `findLiveModel`, `rollbackConfirmMessage`. New `training-jobs/run-history-logic.ts` (+spec) — `sortRunsNewestFirst`, `runStateLabel`, `runProgressLabel`, `formatRunMetric`, `runDatasetLabel`, `hasProducedModel` — shared by both `RunHistoryPage` and `RunDetailPage` (one file, not two: every pure helper the detail page needs is already here, reusing `training-job-logic.ts#jobStateLabel`/`#formatMetric` rather than a third near-identical copy in the same feature folder) — **no separate `run-detail-logic.ts` exists**, a deliberate deviation from the wave's own working plan once it became clear a second file would have had nothing of its own in it.
  - **Dev parity**: `vision.auth.enabled=false`'s dev admin is ADMIN/unbounded, so `orgGuard` (Training tree) and `canAdministerRegistry` (Promote/Roll back) both pass exactly as they do for a real admin.
  - **Not updated**: `core/ui/architecture.spec.ts`'s `ROUTED_PAGES` allowlist (out of this wave's declared write scope) does not yet list `training-jobs/run-history`/`training-jobs/run-detail` — both pages already follow the enforced facade-only-injection shape, so a future pass adding them only tightens the guard, it doesn't change behavior.
- **system-status/** — `/manage/system`'s subsystem grid + overall banner, over `SystemStatusStore`'s `GET /api/system/status` poll. **The banner verdict now names the worst subsystem instead of going a uniform danger red for any one fault (docs/plans/active/OPERATOR-UX-5-PLAN.md finding U3, §2 U3, wave W2).** New `core/system-status/system-status-logic.ts#verdictFor(subsystems, transport)` is a pure `'ok'|'degraded'|'down'` verdict, distinct from the pre-existing `shellStatusSeverity`/`shellStatusLabel` (the sidebar's own worst-of-three rollup, deliberately left unchanged — out of this wave's scope, documented in `verdictFor`'s own doc comment): `'down'` only when the backend itself is unreachable *or* `transport === 'closed'` (the client's own SSE connection, not a backend-reported subsystem); otherwise the banner names `worstFaultySubsystem` by its own label (`Degraded — CV inference down` / `Degraded — Live updates degraded`, the backend's `live-updates` subsystem — a distinct fact from the client transport check above); `'UNKNOWN'` subsystems never lower the verdict (an honest "nothing to report" stays neutral, per this file's own pre-existing `HEALTH_SEVERITY` note); an all-OK/no-fault board stays `'ok'`. `SystemStatusFacade` replaced its old `overall`/`overallSeverity`/`overallMessage` (a bare health-string relabel) with `subsystems`/`verdict`/`overallSeverity`/`overallMessage` built off `verdictFor`; the banner's tone follows the verdict — danger only for `'down'`, warn for `'degraded'`, ok otherwise.
- **maintenance/** (`/fleet/maintenance`, docs/plans/active/WAREHOUSE-UX-PLAN.md §3.3/§4, wave W7 built · wave W9 one-call rewrite) — `MaintenancePage`/`MaintenanceFacade` (page-specific facade, no store — same "not shared across navigation" precedent as `ReportsFacade`/`RosterFacade`): fleet-wide KPI tiles (Grounded/Inspection due/In repair/Retired), a "Ground a vehicle" disclosure form (`VisionApi#setAssetInventory({action:'GROUND', kind, summary})`), an open-records table (Asset/Category/Kind chip/Summary/Opened age/Opened by/Custodian/Close+Release actions), and a "Recently closed" history (last 20). **One fleet-wide read replaces the old per-asset fan-out (wave W9, docs/plans/active/WAREHOUSE-UX-CONTEXT.md "W8 → W9 handoff")** — `MaintenanceFacade.load()` now calls `VisionApi#fleetMaintenance('all')` (`GET /api/maintenance?state=open|closed|all&limit=` → `FleetMaintenanceRecord[]`, W8's new endpoint) once, inside the same blocking `Promise.all` as `listAssets`/`listUsers` — a deliberate honesty change from the pre-W9 per-asset reads' silent per-asset degrade: maintenance records are this page's primary content, not enrichment, so a failed read now shows the page's own error empty-state instead of a quietly-incomplete table. Each `FleetMaintenanceRecord` already carries its own `assetName`/`categoryId`, so the pre-W9 `AssetMaintenanceRow{asset, record}` join type is gone — the record *is* the row; category *display name* and custodian still resolve through the separately-loaded `assets` list (`MaintenanceFacade#categoryNameFor`/`#custodianLabelFor`), mirroring the existing `displayNameFor(userId)` id→name-lookup precedent.
